67b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas bsod nams tshad med namakha' bzhin//'di dag rgyan kun de las rab tu phyung//sa rnams re re'i rgyan rnams gang yod pa//'gro ba kun gyis de dag bsam mi khyab//mi phyed mtshams kyi rdo rje sna
67b2(phal chen): tshogs pa//'khor yangs rnam dag shin tu mnyam pa ste//nor bu'i dra ba sna tshogs kun tu bkram//byang chub shing gi sa'i 'khor kun to//sa phyogs gang na rgyal ba'i gdan yod pa//kha dog mtha'
67b3(phal chen): yas rgya chen sna tshogs te//me tog mngon gtor rin chen rnam par bkram//gser tshon mdzes pas de ni rab tu brgyan//sa'i lha mo de dag rab dga' zhing//skad cig sems kyis mi zad sa yang ston
67b4(phal chen): kun kyang rgyal ba'i zhal du mngon lta zhing//kha dog thams cad rgyan gyi sprin gtong ngo//rin chen sgron ma mang po gsal bar 'bar//spos mang dri zhim mang po rtag tu 'byung//dus dang tshod
67b5(phal chen): kyi rnam pa mang ston cing//lha rnams kun kyang sangs rgyas mtsho da pa byed//phyogs rnams bcu na zhing rnams ji snyed pa//de dag sa rgyan phun sum tshogs yod pa//de dag kun kyang sa
67b6(phal chen): dkyil 'di na snang//sangs rgyas byin rlabs mnyam pa med pas byung//de nas byang chub sems dpa' nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gtsug pud kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/
67b7(phal chen): 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sngan chad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe dag tu//zhing kun phun sum tshogs pa mthong ba'i rnams//
67b8(phal chen): snying po byang chub 'khor gyi sa 'di la//mi zad sa dang de dag rab tu snang//rnam thar yangs pa gang na snang ba yi//sa la rin chen mdzes kun rnam par bkram//rin chen mang pos dkyil 'khor kun tu
68a1(phal chen): @#//gtor//sangs rgyas 'od kyis sprin kyang rab tu snang //sa rnams kun brgyan shing gi dam pa'i drung//kun nas nor bu'i rgyal pos rgyan byas las//bsod nams 'od kyi dbyangs kyi 
68a2(phal chen): 'brug sgra 'byung //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul tshul yang rab tu brjod//rin chen mang po sgron ma'i char phab cing//rin chen mdzes par bkod pa rnam par mdzes//sgra dbyangs kun gyi 'brug sgra shin tu sgrog/sa'i lha 
68a3(phal chen): rnams se yang yang dag ston//rin chen 'od 'phro sgron ma rnam par snang//'od zer sprin ni sa las rab tu byung //nor bu dra ba sil ma kun nas bkram//rin chen sna tshogs rigs kyis yan lag brgyan//sa rnams
68a4(phal chen): kun nas mdzes pa 'di dag ltos//rin chen mang po tshon bzang brgyana du bkye//'gro ba dag la chos kyi rgya mtsho sgrog/de dag chos nyid rnam par dag pa mthong//ma lus phyogs na sangs rgyas 
68a5(phal chen): thams cad kyi//byang chub shing gi phun sum tshogs pa dag/sa'i dkyil 'khor 'di na kun snang ste//de dag chos nyid rnam par dag pa brjod//sangs rgyas gdan las chos rnams
68a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho yi//chos sgo bde bar gshegs pas gang gsungs pa//sems can kun gyi bsam pa ji bzhin du//sa 'khor rnams kyang kun nas rab tu 'byung //dri zhim 'od rnams rtag tu 'byung bas na//
68a7(phal chen): de dag ji bzhin snod kyi 'gro la 'gengs//srog chags rnams kyi nyon mongs zhi bar byed//sa 'khor de nas de 'dra rab tu sgrog/sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul phun sum gang dag dang //dri medas ni gang gis mdzes
68a8(phal chen): gyur pa'i//phun sum tshogs pa'i rgyan rnams re re zhing//bskal pa bye ba dag tu'ang brjod mi nus//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes chen po'i nyi mas rab tu gnon pa'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi 
68b1(phal chen): mthus 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//'jig rten kun gyi bsam pa tshim mdzad cing//sangs rgyas chos kyi gzhal med khang na mdzes//
68b2(phal chen): 'byam klas phyogs rnams kun tu mngon sum du//sangs rgyas 'gro gtso gzhal med khang na snang//rin chen kun gyis snying po rab tu brgyan//rgyan rnams kun gyis de bzhin mdzes shing 'bar//sangs 
68b3(phal chen): rgyas pho brang 'od ni bsam mi khyab//de na bzhugs shing sangs rgyas rab tu mdzes//ka ba'i rgyan ni nor bu sna tshogs te//phyogs rnams kun sgor rta babs sprin kyang bres//stegs bu'i phreng ba kun nas rgyan du 
68b4(phal chen): bkod//gser gyi dril bu'i sprin kyang rnam par brgyus//me tog bla re rnam pa sna tshogs ltos//rin chen shing sdong dar gyi bla re bres//nor bu thams cad sgo nas phreng ba 'bul//de na ye shes 
68b5(phal chen): rgya mtsho rab tu mdzes//spos dri rgyal mtshan rin chen dra ba ste//'od zer sgron ma'i sprin gyis kun tu yog/rgyan rnams sna tshogs kun nas shin tu bres//de na sangs rgyas rab tu 'phags 
68b6(phal chen): pa bzhugs//phyogs rnams kun sgo sprul pa'i sprin gyi rnams//dbyangs kyis 'jig rten khyab par grags byed cing//'byam klas phyogs kyi sems can rnams 'dul ba//sangs rgyas pho brang 'di na de 
68b7(phal chen): dag grag/me tog shing dang rin chen spungs spel ba//'phags par phyogs rnams kun tu mngon par mdzes//de la dus gsum zhing rnams thams cad kyang//gzugs brnyan gyur par de dag rab tu snang//
68b8(phal chen): phyogs rnams kun tu rin chen mang bkye ba//de dag sna tshogs mdzes par rnam par 'bar//phyogs rnams kun tu skar khung mdzes par brgyan//rta babs mda' yab sna tshogs mang pos brgyan//de la 
69a1(phal chen): @#//dri med 'od zer kun nas snang ba yi//sangs rgyas pho brang rnam pa bsam yas te//gzhal med khang mang thams cad de la snang//de dag kun na'ang rgyal ba bzhugs shing mdzes//
69a2(phal chen): phyogs rnams kun na 'khor rnams ji snyed pa//rgyal ba'i pho brang 'dir ni kun zhugs kyang//mtha' dang dbung med mu'ang de bzhin med//rang byung sangs rgyas pho brang de na bzhugs//de nas byang chub sems
69a3(phal chen): dpa' yon tan rin po che bsam gyis mi khyab pa ye shes rgya mtsho'i dpal gyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho yod pa de dag thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa
69a4(phal chen): 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas zhing rdul shin tu phra snyed kyi//bsod nams rgya mtsho rnam par rab sbyangs te//sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul smon lam yang dag byung//snying po byang chub dri med rnam par
69a5(phal chen): mdzes//spos zhim thams cad rtsa nas yang dag rgyu//yid bzhina nor mchog rgyal pos kun nas brgyan//rdo rje phye ma'i nor bu rgyal pos bris//shing sdong rtsa ba rin chen kun las byas//
69a6(phal chen): rdo rje phye ma'i nor bu 'khor lo 'byung//lhun rnams thams cad rin chen rgyan gyis brgyan//yal ga stug po sprin ltar kun nas 'khyil//snying po byang chub de na rgyal ba mdzes//
69a7(phal chen): yal ga dog pa kun nas mngon pa ste//'khor rnams snum 'jam phyogs rnams kun tu 'phags//me tog 'bras bu rgyun rnams kun kyang 'gyed//snying po byang chub rgya che bsam mi khyab//'od rnams
69a8(phal chen): bkye ba phyogasu rnam par mdzes//dri ma med pa'i 'od 'bar zad mi shes//yal ga'i sgo nas sprin kun char yang 'bebs//snying po byang chub smon lam yangs pas byung//me tog nor dang rin chen mi zad nor//
69b1(phal chen): mu tig nor dag phyogs dang phyogs rnams kun nas brgyus//phye ma nor gyi gnas kyi sprin 'gyed de//snying po byang chub phyogs rnams kun tu 'byin//bde gshegs snying po byang chub 'di la ltos//pad mo'i dra ba nor bu
69b2(phal chen): dri med mang//rin chen 'od zer dkyil 'khor kun nas 'phro//dril bu'i dra ba'i sprin gyi sgra dbyangs 'byin//phyogs bcu'i shing bzang gzugs rnams gang yod pa//'byam klas zhing gi ma lus thams cad
69b3(phal chen): dag/byang chub shing la de dag kun nas snang//de drung sangs rgyas dri med rnam par mdzes//yal ga kun nas rin chen mi zad nor//byang chub sems dpa' dag kyang stag 'byung zhing//bsod nams rgya chen dkyil
69b4(phal chen): 'khor char phab ste//phyogs bcur rgyal ba mchod phyir de dag 'dong //de bzhin gshegs pa sngan chad cir gyur dang //byang chub spyod las byung ba 'di dag ltos//yal ga kun las rin chen nor mi
69b5(phal chen): zad sangs rgyas yul kun bsam gyis mi khyab bo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' mig brgya pa pad+mo sna tshogs kyi gtsug pud kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor rgya mtsho yod pa
69b6(phal chen): thams cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//dus gsum gshegs pa'i rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul ba//de dag rin chen sgo nas kun kyang grag/de ni mtha' dang dbung yang med pa
69b7(phal chen): ste//snying po byang chub 'di la kun snang ngo//me tog mang po'i phreng ba sbyangs bkye zhing//'od zer sprin gyis phyogs rnams kun bkang ste//byang chub shing la gnas pa'i lha tshogs rnams//sangs rgyas
69b8(phal chen): lta zhing rtag tu mchod pa byed//spos kyi rgyal mtshan nor bu sna tshogs 'bar//spos kyi 'od 'phro bdug spos rtag tu 'byung//'khor gyi rgya mtsho kun la khyab par zhim//shing gi rgyal po de phyir phyogasu
70a1(phal chen): @#//mdzes//pad+mo 'phrul ba stug cing gser mdog 'dra//rgyal ba'i sprin sgra rtag par rab tu 'gyed//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams ma lus khyab byas shing //sems can rnams kyi 
70a2(phal chen): nyon mongs gdung ba sel//rgyal ba'i 'khor ni tshad med rgya che ste//phyogs rnams kun nas snying po byang chub la//gzugs brnyan snang zhing dri med 'od mchog 'gyed//byang chub shing gi rnam 'phrul 'di dag ltos//
70a3(phal chen): smon lam sgra dang dbyangs kyi mar me rnams//rin chen sna tshogs yal ga kun las 'bar//srid pa rgya mtsho kun na sngan chad du//rgyal ba'i spyod pa ji ltar byung ba ston//byang chub lha rnams zhing gi rdul snyed 
70a4(phal chen): de//de dag thams cad snying po byang chub gnas//thub pa'i byang chub shing la skad cig cing //rnam thar gzhan dang gzhan mang de dag ston//rang byung spyad pa thams cad spyod pa'i tshe//sngan 
70a5(phal chen): chad de bzhin gshegs pa gang mchod pa//de dag mtshan bcas sngon gyi sbyor bcas pa//thams cad rin chen sgo nas rab tu snang //phyogs bcu'i sems can ji snyed snod gyur pa//de 
70a6(phal chen): dag thams cad 'di las sgra 'byung ste//de dag cis 'dul rnam dag ci 'dra ba'ang //snying po byang chub 'di las kun brjod do//byang chub shing rnams mtha' yas phyogs kun na//bsam gyis mi khyab rgyan gyi 
70a7(phal chen): sprin can dag/mtha' yas sna tshogs rgyan can thams cad kyang //sngan chad de bzhin gshegs pas yongs sbyangs pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' gser gyi 'od 'phro gzi brjid dkyil 'khor gyi 'od 
70a8(phal chen): kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//srid pa'i rgya mtshor byang chub spyod mdzad che//mos pa'i 
70b1(phal chen): dkyil 'khor yongasu smon dag cing//gnas dang gnas min stobs rnams thugs chud pa'i//bder gshegs sa stobs rnam par dag pa mthong//bskal pa kun tu rgya mtsho las sbyangs te//mnyam nyid chos 
70b2(phal chen): bsgoms ji ltar bsgrubs pa'i rnams//sangs rgyas 'od las de bzhin phyogs bkang zhing//las rnams rgya mtsho de bzhin rab tu brjod//sna tshogs thabs kyis 'byam klas stobs sbyong tshe//bskal pa snga 
70b3(phal chen): mar 'gro ba'i dbang po bzhin//yongasu smin byas de bas 'khor kun kyang//sems dag sangs rgyas dbang po'i stobs la 'jug/'jig rten kun gyi mos pa sna tshogs dang //bsam pa ji bzhin sbyor ba rnam 
70b4(phal chen): spyod pa//de dag ji bzhin rgyal ba chos ston te//rang byung mos pa mkhyen pa'i stobs ston to//phyogs rnams ma lus zhing rnams rgya mtsho dang //'byam klas sems can khams dang 
70b5(phal chen): chos kyi khams//namakha'i khams rnams mnyam nyid mkhyen mi rtog/rang byung spu rtser khams mkhyen stobs kyang ston//bder gshegs ye shes rgya chen kun tu 'gro/skad cig 
70b6(phal chen): gcig gis dus gsum ma lus 'gengs//phyogs dang zhing dang bskal pa 'gro bkang zhing//sems can kun la stobs 'jug mngon par bstan//bsam gtan rnam thar stobs sa mtha' dbung med//
70b7(phal chen): ting 'dzin rgya mtsho'i tshul ni 'gro kun snyed//thams cad rgyal ba'i sku la bstan pas na//sems can bsam pa'i gnas kyi nyon mongs sel//sangs rgyas ye shes dus gsum bsgribs pa med//
70b8(phal chen): skad cig kun la dus gsum spu rtser ston//zhing dang sems can sangs rgyas de bzhin chos//de ltar rgyal bas rjesu dran stobs bstan//thub pa'i spyan ni namakha' mnyam pa ste//des ni chos dbyings ma 
71a1(phal chen): @#//lus gsal bar gzigs//thams cad tshul gyis chags med gzhal yas sa//brnyes pas ye shes spyan ni rnam mang bstan//'jig rten kun gyi sbyor mang ji snyed
71a2(phal chen): dang //'gro ba'i bag chags bag nyal ma lus par//thabs sgo kun nas nyon mongs zhi mdzad cing //rgyal ba 'jig rten gang bar kun nas byung //de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings kun nas
71a3(phal chen): sgrog pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//rgya chen sangs rgyas byin rlabs phyogs kun tu//sangs rgyas
71a4(phal chen): ston kyang rtog pa mi mnga' ste//pha rol phyin pa rgya chen yongs bkang ba//byang chub spyod pa rgya chen de dag snang //'gro ba rnams la thugs rje che bskyed cing //sbyin pa'i mthar phyin sngan
71a5(phal chen): chad spyad pas na//de phyir thub pa'i sku ni mtshan gyis rgyan//'gro ba kun gyis blta zhing dga' ba skye//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang po mtha' yas sngon//tshul khrims pha rol phyin pa
71a6(phal chen): yongs sbyangs pas//de phyir bskal pa phyogasu dri med cing //shin tu 'phags par 'jig rten sdug bsngal sel//ma log mnyam pa nyid la dad bskyed de//sngan chad bzod pa'i dkyil 'khor rnam sbyangs
71a7(phal chen): pas//'gro dang mtshungs mnyam yon tan kun ston te//de bas phyogasu 'od rnams shin tu 'phro//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang bor sngon spyod tshe//'gro ba rnams kyi bsgribs pa thibs po bstsal//de
71a8(phal chen): phyir phyogs bcu'i 'gro ba kun bkang ste//shing rab drung na rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//bsam gtan yan lag rgya mtsho sbyong ba'i tshe//bskal pa yangs par rgyal bas rab tu spyad//de bas 'gro
71b1(phal chen): ba'i gzhal med dga' ba skyed//nyon mongs dri ma bsgrib pa kun kyang sel//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho'i drung du sngon spyod cing//shes rab mthar phyin rgyal bas sngon bkang ste//'gro ba'i gti mug mun pa yong+sa 
71b2(phal chen): su bsal//de bas rgyal ba kun tu rnam par mdzes//'gro ba kun mnyam thabs sgo thams cad kyis//yang dag byung zhing 'gro ba rnam sbyangs pas//phyogs rnams kun na kun la mngon par bzhugs//
71b3(phal chen): phyi ma'i bskal par 'gro ba mngon du lta//smon lam pha rol phyin pa rab sbyong tshe//bskal pa rgya mtsho shin tu rgya che zhing//phyi mtha'i bskal par 'gro ba'i sprin mnyam par//spyad pas de phyir 'gro ba kun na 
71b4(phal chen): mdzes//rgyal bas chos kun stobs kyi mthar phyin pa//bskal pa gzhal yas mang por yongasu sbyangs//de phyir rang byung stobs kyi pha rol phyin//phyogs kyi zhing rnams kun na de phyir 
71b5(phal chen): 'phags//thams cad mkhyen nyid namakha' mtshungs mnyam pa'i//ye shes kun gyi sgo nas sngon sbyangs pas//de phyir chags med ye shes pha rol phyin//phyogs bcu kun 
71b6(phal chen): na rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sprin yid du 'ong ba dri ma med pa'i zla bas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe 
71b7(phal chen): tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul yul ni phyogs rnams kyi//'gro ba'i rgyud dbyings namakha' mnyam par snang//sa rnams yangs pa ji ltar sngon bskyed pa//de bzhin kun 
71b8(phal chen): kyang rin chen sgo nas brjod//dang po'i sa ni dga' ba'i shugs kyi yul//phyogs dbyings kun gyi bder gshegs rnams mthong zhing//gzhal med bskal par dri med yongs sbyangs pas//chos 
72a1(phal chen): @#//dbyings yangs la ye shes rab tu byung //thams cad chos la dri med sa gnyis te//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang por rnam spyad cing//rgyal ba tshad med rgya mtsho mchod byas nas//
72a2(phal chen): 'gro ba tshad med dri med sbyangs pas byung//bsod nams dkyil 'khor 'od ni sa gsum ste//snying po bzod pa'i dkyil 'khor lhag mthong brtan//gang du de skyes rin chen sgo dag nas//chos kyi
72a3(phal chen): sprin ni rgya chen rab tu thos//'od 'phro rgya mtsho ldan pa gzhal med sa//mthu'i 'khor 'od snying po snying rje ste//zhing rnams kun tu sgrib dang mnyam pa'i lus//'gro ba'i nyon mongs kun nas rnam sbyangs
72a4(phal chen): pa'o//dka' rgyal snying po mnyam dbyings mnyam nyid de//zhing rnams kun tu 'gro zhing zhi la gzhol//sangs rgyas chos yul kun nas rab tu skyes//de yang rnam dag ji bzhin rgyal bas bstan//
72a5(phal chen): shes rab rgya mtsho yangs pa'i sprin la spyod//'byam klas chos kyi mthar thug rnam pa kun//zhing rnams kun la mngon sum namakha' 'dra//de 'byung byang chub shing chen 'di las grag/
72a6(phal chen): chos dbyings mkha' dang 'dra ba'i lus can te//'gro ba kun 'gengs 'gro ba kun snang byed//'byam klas thabs kyi sgo nas rnam par dag/ring du song mchog de ni rgyal bas bstan//thams cad chos dang 
72a7(phal chen): smon lam spyod mang dang //zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i dbyings kyang mnyam rtogs pas//bskal pa kun tu 'gro mchog mi g.yo ste//gzhal med sa de'i sprin kyang rab tu sgrog/tshad med yul can stobs 
72a8(phal chen): kyis rab tu snang//rdzu 'phrul stobs kyis 'gro zhing stobs la 'jug/'di ni rdul med dge ba'i blo gros sa//bskal pa rgya mtshor byang chub shing las brjod//chos kyi sprin ni rgya chen sa bcu ste//
72b1(phal chen): dus rnams kun 'gengs namakha'i rang bzhin 'dra//sangs rgyas yul gyi sgra dang dbyangs kun tu//rgyal ba'i byin gyis 'di las rab tu grag/de nas byang chub sems dpa' rnam par gnon pa gti mug med pa'i
72b2(phal chen): 'od zer gyi rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//rnam pa du mar 'khor rnams gnas pa ni//dad pa sna tshogs
72b3(phal chen): yongasu dag pa'i sems//rgyan dang yul rnams kun la rab tu zhugs//de dag sangs rgyas ye shes 'jug par 'gyur//phan tshun smon lam rnam dag spyod yul te//
72b4(phal chen): dag pa mtha' yas bya ba rab tu byas//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam 'phrul thams cad kyang//gzhan dang gzhan gyi sgo nas rab tu mthong//bder gshegs chos kyi sku la zhugs pa dag/
72b5(phal chen): la las rgyal ba'i chos sku de nyid la//kun tu sprin 'dra gzhal med chags med pa'i//mtha' yas chos kyi rang bzhin chos nyid mthong//la las rgyal ba'i gzugs sku de nyid la//
72b6(phal chen): phyogs bcu kun tu rnam par 'phrul mdzad cing//yon tan mtha' yas 'od sbyin rab 'bar ba//'gro ba dad tshul sprin du gyur par mthong//la la rgyal ba'i ye shes chags med 'jug/dus gsum mnyaM
72b7(phal chen): nyid mkha' dang 'dra ba'i sku//'gro ba kun gyi bsam pa'i tshul 'byung ba'i//sangs rgyas stobs la rab tu 'jug par mthong//sangs rgyas gsung ni thogs pa med 'byung ste//phyogs kun zhing 
72b8(phal chen): rnams ma lus rgyas 'gyur zhing//'gro ba snod bzhin skul bar byed pa yi//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung la la la 'jug/la la sangs rgyas snang ba sna tshogs 'jug/sna tshogs snang bas 'gro ba
73a1(phal chen): @#//kun bkang zhing//rgyal ba'i rdzu 'phrul kun tu snang mdzad pa'i//sangs rgyas 'od ni la las der yang mthong//ba spu'i sgo nas 'od zer rab bkye ba'i//'od zer rgya mtsho'i
73a2(phal chen): sprin la la la 'jug/sngan chad rgyal ba'i spyod pa bstan pa la//la la dga' zhing rgyal ba shes par byed//la las bsod nams sna tshogs mtshan gyi rnams//bder gshegs bsod nams byung ba 
73a3(phal chen): ji bzhin mthong//pha rol phyin pa rgya mtsho sbyong ba'i tshe//ji ltar dag gyur mtshan kyang la las mthong//sgo rnams kun nas rgyal ba tshad med de//mtha' dang dbung med chos kyi dbyings bkang zhing//
73a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas   byin rlabs rab tu brjod pa kun//rnam 'aphrul sangs rgyas yul ni la las mthong//de nas sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis brlabs kyis//'jig rten gyi khams

84a7(phal chen): kun nas yog par byin gyi brlabs par byed cing/bcom ldan 'das gang na ba der phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/'og gi phyogs su brten nas seng ge'i khri rin po che'i sgron ma rab tu 
84a8(phal chen): 'bar ba 'byung ba'i snying po can dag mngon par sprul nas skyil mo dkrung bcas te 'khod do//de nas yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya  mtsho gzhi dang /snying po me tog gi rgyan gyis brgyan pa 'di dang mdab chags
84b1(phal chen): su/steng gi phyogs na 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi rgya mtsho'i rgyan ces bya ba yod de/'jig rten gyi khams bar mi' rgyud de dag las 'jig rten gyi khams mtshan yid du 'ong ba 
84b2(phal chen): rnam par snang ba zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa yon tan dang grags pa thogs pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa rab tu snang ba'i rgyal po bzhugs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho nas/byang chub sems dpa'
84b3(phal chen): stobs dang /brtson 'grus chags pa med pa'i blo gros shes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang lhan cig tu 'phags nas/bcom ldan 'das gang na ba 
84b4(phal chen): der 'ong bar gyur te/rin po che kha dog mtha' yas par 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcus namakha' kun nas yog par byin gyis brlob cing /nor bu rin po che kun nas 'od kyi dra ba 'od 'phro ba'i sprin btsus 
84b5(phal chen): namakha' kun nas yog par byan gyis brlabs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan rgya chen pos rnam par snang bar 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcas namakha' kun nas yog 
84b6(phal chen): bar byin gyis brlabs pa dang /spos thams cad 'phro ba'i sprin bcus mkha' kun nas yog par byon gyis brlabs pa dang /rgyan thams cad 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcos namakha' kun nas yog pa byin
84b7(phal chen): gyis brlabs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bces nas kha' kun nas yog par byinagyis brlabs pa dang /shing dang ze ba rnam pa sna tshogasu 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcis 
84b8(phal chen): namakha' kun nas yog bar byin gyis brlabs pa dag /rdo rje sna tshogs 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcusanamakha' kun nas yog par byin gyis brlabs pa da /nor bu'i rgyal po byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa 
85a1(phal chen): @#//mtha' yas pa brjod cing 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcus namakha' kun nas yog par byin gyis brlabs pa dang /mu tig gi sgron ma 'od 'phro ba'i sprin bcu namakha' kun nas yog
85a2(phal chen): par byin gyis brlabs pa dag /de dag la sogs pa 'od 'phro ba'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis namakha' kun nas yog bar byin gyis brlabs par byed cing /bcom ldan 
85a3(phal chen): 'das gang na bder phyin te/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal nas/steng gi phyogasu brten te/seng ge'i khri sangs rgyas kyi gsung sgrog pa'i 'od zer 'byung ba'i pad+mo'i snying po can dag mngon par
85a4(phal chen): sprul nas/skyil mo dkrung bcas te 'khod do//de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye ba phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snye nas kyang /byang chub 
85a5(phal chen): sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bye baphrag bcu'i rdul shin tu/phra ba snyed byang chub sems dpa' re re zhing yang byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba
85a6(phal chen): snyed kyis bskor cing mdun du byas te/byang chub sems dpa' re ri zhing yang rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs kyi mchod pa'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis namakha' kun nas
85a7(phal chen): yog par byin gyis brlob cing 'ongs par gyur te phyin nas gang nas 'ongs pa'i phyogasu brten te/seng ge khri rin po che sna tshogs kyi rgyan gyi snying po can dag mngon bar sprul cing /skyil mo
85a8(phal chen): dkrung bcas te 'khod par gyur to//'khod pa'i rjes la byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi ba spu'i khung bu thams cad re re nas 'od zer gyi sprin rin po che thams cad ltar kha dog sna tshogs pa'i 'jig
85b1(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed rab tu byung bar gyur to//rin po che re re'i sgo nas kyang byang chub sems dpa' seng ge'i khri pad+mo'i snying po can la 'khod pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho bcu brdul
85b2(phal chen): shin tu phra bsnyed rab tu byung bar gyur te//byang chub sems dpa' re res kyang chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul rgya mtsho'i khams kyi rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad kyi sbubs gang bar byas nas/rdul shin tu phra ba re re la
85b3(phal chen): yang zhing 'byam klas pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra bsnyed du rab tu 'jug par 'gyur to//zhing re rer yang dus gsum du gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad mthong zhing shes par
85b4(phal chen): gyur to//sems kyi skad cig re re la yang rmi lam lta bu'i rnam par 'phrul pa yang dag par bstan pa'i sgo dag nas yang dag par bskul ba byed par gyur to//sems kyi skad gcig re re la yang
85b5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed re re'i gnas thams cad du skye ba dang 'chi 'pho ba yang dag par bstan pa 'i sgo dag nas yang dag bar skul ba byed par gyur to//sems
85b6(phal chen): kyi skad cig re re la yang 'jig rten gyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad shin tu bstan pa'i sgo dag nas dag par skul bar gyur to//sems kyi ska cig re re
85b7(phal chen): la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra bsnyed kyi 'byam klas pa thams cad rab tu bskyod pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rab tu brjod cing rnam par 'phrul ba'i sgo dag nas yang dag pa skul ba byed
85b8(phal chen): bar gyur to//sems kyi skud cig re re la 'jig rten gyi khams rgyam tsho'i rdul shin tu phra pa snyed kyi /sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongasu dag par bya ba dang /smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bsgrag pa'i
86a1(phal chen): sgo dag nas yang dag par bskul ba byed par gyur to//sems kyi skad cig re re la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'gro ba thams cad kyi nges pa'i tshig dang /
86a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi yan lag rnam par bstan cing brjod pa'i sgo dag nas/yang dag par skul ba byed par gyur to//sems kyi skad cig re re 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyedakyi sangs
86a3(phal chen): rgyas kyi chos kyi sprin thams cad kyi char dbab pa'i sgo dag nas yang dag par skul ba byedapar gyur to//sems kyi skang cig re re la 'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shon tu phra ba snyed kyi phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten
86a4(phal chen): gyi khamasu chos kyi dbyings snang bas rgyas par rnam par 'phrul ba'i mthu'i sgo dag nas yang dag bar skul ba byed par gyur do//sems kyi skad cig rere la 'jig rten gyi khams mgo mtsho'i rdul shin tu phrab bsnyed kyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul
86a5(phal chen): shin tu phra ba snyed kyi/sangs rgyas kyi skus chos kyi dbyings gang bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa dang /'o do thams cad kyi sgo dag nas yang dag pa ra skul ba byed par gyur  to//
86a6(phal chen): sems kyi skad cig re rel 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snye kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad na byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po rab tu gnas pa'i sgo dag nas yang
86a7(phal chen): dag par bskul ba byed par gyur to//de lta bu'i chos kyi dbyings rgyas par bkang ste/sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du yang dag par skul ba byed cing/'jig rten gyi khams 'byam klas
86a8(phal chen): pa dag tu rab tu bdal te/zhing re re yang sems kyi skad cig re re la/sems can ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi ngan song gi rgyud du gtogs pa'i sdug bsngal thams cad ni rab tu zhi bar byed do//
86b1(phal chen): sems can phyin ci log la nges pa ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed ni yang dag par nges pa'i phung po la rab tu 'god do//sems can ri rab kyi rdul shin tu pra bsnyed ni dge ba dang yid du 'ong ba dang /mtho ris su skyes pa la
86b2(phal chen): rab tu 'god do//sems pa can ri rab kyi rdul shing tu pra ba snyed ni nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi sa la rab tu 'god do//sems can ri rab kyi rdus shin tu phra ba snyed ni dge ba'i bshes bsnyen bkur ba'i bsod
86b3(phal chen): nams kyi tshogs la rab tu 'god do/zhing de dag re rer yang sems can ri rab kyi rdu shin tu phra ba snyed ni sngan chad ma skyed pa'i byang chub kyi sems skye bar byed do//zhing de dag re rer yang /sems can ri rab kyi
86b4(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed/byang chub sems dpa'i sa phyir mi ldog pa yang dag par thob pa la yang dag par skul lo//zhing de dag re rer rang sems can ri rab kyi dang dang /de
86b5(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi spyan chos thams cad kun nas snang zhing gzigs pa la yongasu sbyangs te/rab tu 'god do//zhing de dag rer rang sems can ri bab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba
86b6(phal chen): snyed stobs thams cad kyis rab tu gnas pa dang /ye shes kyi tshogs zad mi shes pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya ba'i smon lam rgya mtsho la rab tu 'god do//zhing /de dag re rer yang
86b7(phal chen): sems can ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi gdung dang rgyud yang dag par 'byung ba'i gnas rgya mtsho rnam par spyod pa la rab tu 'god do/byang chub sems dpa'
86b8(phal chen): de dag 'od zer gyi sgo thams cad nas kyang /tshigasu bcad pa 'di lta bu dag byung bar gyur to//'od zer thams cad sgo nas sgra 'byung ste//ji snyed byang chub spyod par 'jug pa dang //rgyal sras yon tan phun sum
87a1(phal chen): @#//gang yod pa'i//dbyangs kyis phyogs rnams kun tu bkang zhing sgrog/bskal pa rgya mtshor skyo med spyad pa spyod//sdug bsngal kun las 'gro ba kun 'grel kyang//yid la skye med 
87a2(phal chen): zhum pa gang yang med//'di'i tshul la rgyal sras rab tu zhugs//chos kyi sgo rnams kun la rab spyod cing//tshul 'dir bskal pa rgya mtsho zad par byed//mtha' med dbung med lhag ma gang yang med//de dag chos 
87a3(phal chen): nyid zhi ba rab tu sgrog/dus gsum rgyal ba'i smon lam gang yod pa//rgyal sras de dag 'gro la phan don du//de dag thams cad ma lus yongasu sbyong//de dag spyod pa'i rgyan ni 'di 'dra'o//rgyal ba kun gyi 
87a4(phal chen): 'khor du nyer phyin te//phyogs rnams kun na gzugs brnyan lus 'chang zhing//rgyal sras ye shes rgya mtsho'i blo gros can//de dag chos nyid zhir 'jug 'di 'dra'o//'od zer sgo rnams re rer mtha'
87a5(phal chen): yas pa'i//bsam yas zhing rnams de dag rab tu 'jug/spyod pas mig ni kun nas yongs dag ste//rgyal sras de dag spyod yul 'di yin no//skra rtse gcig tu de dag rab gnas shing//
87a6(phal chen): zhing rnams dbyings kun kun nas bskyod byas kyang//sems can rnams la 'jigs par mi byed de//sa 'di rnam dag tshul 'chang dag gi'o//tshul rnams kun la bsam gyis mi khyab lus//de dag kun tu 
87a7(phal chen): zhing rnams sna tshogs sprul//skad cig sems la 'chi 'pho skye ba snang//rgyan 'di sems la chags med rnams kyi'o//dus gsum bskal pa gang yod ma lus par//skad cig gcig gis thams 
87a8(phal chen): cad rab tu ston//rgyu ma lta bu lus med dngos kyang med//de 'dra'i chos nyid chags med blos rtogaso//bzang po'i spyod pa mchog la zhugs pa rnams//'gro ba kun la yid du 'ong ba'i lus//
87b1(phal chen): rgyal sras de dag chos nyid 'di yin te//'od zer kun gyi sgo nas dbyangs kyang sgrog/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' mtha' dang dbung med pa de dag/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la rnam par 
87b2(phal chen): spyod pas rnam par 'phrul ba thob par bya ba'i phyir/smin mtshams kyi mdzod spu nas//'od zer chen po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad ye shes kyi snang bas phyogs rnam par ston pa'i snying po zhes bya ba rab tu 
87b3(phal chen): bkye'o//'od zer rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog sgron ma sprin du 'dal ba/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rab tu snang ba de byung nas/tshul thams cad kyi phyogs 'byam klas pa rab tu snang bar byas te/
87b4(phal chen): phyogs thams cad kyi dbyings rgya mtsho rnams rab tu snang bar byas nas/sems can rgya mtsho rnams rab tu snangabar byas te/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bstan nas/
87b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi zhing mang po thams cad rab tu bskyod pa'i rgyan gyi tshul thams cad kyis rab tu bskyod nas/sangs rgyas kyi sprin 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 
87b6(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad dang mnyam pa dag tu rab tu spros nas/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang mos pa ji lta ba rgya mtsho dag tu/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i 
87b7(phal chen): sprin gyi char dus gsum gyi rdul shin tu phra ba dang mnyam pa phab ste/dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho rab tu bstan nas/sems can thams can ma lus par 'khor ba 
87b8(phal chen): thams cad las rab tu gdon par bya ba'i phyir mos pa ji bzhin du 'byung ba dang 'thun pa'i sprin gyi char rab tu dbab pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi smon lam ma lus pa'i sprin gyi char rab du 
88a1(phal chen): @#//dbab pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i 'khor dag tu dga' bar phrad pa byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la g.yas phyogs
88a2(phal chen): su bskor ba byas te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar pad mo chen po chu ba rin po che thams cad las bsgrubs pa nang rim nor bu'i rgyal po'i mdzod sna tshogs las byas pa/lo ma rin po che thams cad kyi char 
88a3(phal chen): 'bebs pa/ze ba'i phreng ba'i rgyan spos rin po che thams cad la sa byas pa/snying po 'dzam+bu chu klung gi gser gyi tshon gyis rab tu mdzes par bris pa/'od kyi 'khor rnam par dag cing 'od zer gyi dra bas rnam 
88a4(phal chen): par snang bar byas pa'i sprin dang ldan pa/rgyan thams cad rab tu 'byung ba/sems kyi skad cig la sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' yas pa ston pa'i sgra rab tu 'byung ba/nor bu'i 
88a5(phal chen): rgyal po mtha' yas pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi sku'i gzugs brnyan gyi sprin gyis thams cad du gang bar byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /smon lam thams cad rab tu 
88a6(phal chen): ston pa'i dbyangs 'byin par gyur te byungo//pad+mo chen po de byung ba'i rjes la byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad la pham med pa'i dbyangs shes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi khams 
88a7(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das kyi mdzod spu de nyid nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das la lan du mar g.yas phyogasu bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/
88a8(phal chen): pad+mo chen po de'i snying po la 'dug go/byang chub sems dpa' 'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phrab snyed po de dag kyang ze ba'i phreng ba de dag la 'khod do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos
88b1(phal chen): thams cad la pham pa med pa'i dbyangs chos kyi dbyings rgya che zhing tshad med pas rab tu dga' ba'i shugs shin tu skyes pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul gyi rjesu song ba'i ye shes can//sangs rgyas kyi
88b2(phal chen): chos kyi sku rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pa la rab tu zhugs pa'i mthu dang ldan pa/zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pa/ba spu thams cad las sprul pa'i
88b3(phal chen): sprin zad mi shes pas kun tu khebs par byed pa/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la dmigs pa thams cad chos kyi dbyings kun tu sgyur ba/chos kyi dbyings thams cad la dus gsum khyab par byed pa'i
88b4(phal chen): spyod yul gyis rnam par spyod pa/phyogs thams cad kyi de bazhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis brlabs 'chang ba/tinge 'dzin thams cad la rab tu 'jog cing phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thug
88b5(phal chen): par tinge 'dzin dang /rnam par thar pa la rnam par spyod pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa thams cad dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi lus dang ldan pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
88b6(phal chen): rdzu 'phrul dang /rnam par 'phrul ba mtha' dang dbung med pa la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas sku ni chos kyi dbyings kyis gsal//'gro ba kun la rgyal ba mngon sum 
88b7(phal chen): ste||ji ltar mos pa'i sems can rgyas bkang nas//snying po byang chub de la rgyal ba bzhugs//zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran mchog snyed kyi//rgyal ba spu rtse kun la rab bzhugs te//byang chub sems dpa'i 
88b8(phal chen): 'khor gyis kun nas bskor//kun kyang bzang po'i spyod pa mchog ston to//spu gcig rtse la zhing mang rgya mtsho ste//byang chub snying por pad+mo gdan la bzhugs//chos dbyings rgya chen thams cad kun bkang 
89a1(phal chen): @#//ste||spu rnams kun la rnam par 'phrul ba ston//zhing rnams re re dag na rab bzhugs shing//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun tu rgyas mdzad pas//phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems 
89a2(phal chen): dpa'i sprin gyi rnams//kun kyang snying po byang chub drung du 'dong//zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran mchog snyed kyi//byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho'i 'od//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khor du nye bar 'dong//chos
89a3(phal chen): dbyings 'byam klas rtag tu rgyas par 'gengs//zhing rnams thams cad gzugs brnyan rab tu ston//chos dbyings rdul rnams ye shes spyod yul can//kun kyang bzang po'i spyod la rab tu gnas//sangs 
89a4(phal chen): rgyas kun gyi 'khor du yang dag byung//byang chub sems dpa' ye shes mchog yul can//chos sprin nyan cing rab tu mkhas pa'i rnams//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khaur rnams kun na gnas//zhing
89a5(phal chen): rnams kun tu bskal pa rgya mtshor spyod//rgya mtshor chos la rnam par snang bar spyod//de dag rgyal ba'i sa rnams rgya mtshor zhugs//sangs rgyas chos rnams gzhan dang gzhan 'byung 
89a6(phal chen): ba||de dag smon lam rgya mtshor rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas yon tan legs pa rgya mtsho dang //kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa de dag shes//sangs rgyas chos kyi dbyings rnams kun bkang zhing //de dag rnam 
89a7(phal chen): 'phrul rgya chen kun la zhugs//zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran mang snyed kyi//lus kyi sprin ni rtag par rab tu ston//sems can kun bkang 'od kyis kun la skyob//bsam pa yid bzhin chos kyi char yang 
89a8(phal chen): 'bebs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dpal pad+mo rnam par lta ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//
89b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas ye shes shin tu zab//dus rnams kun tu de 'byung ste//chos dbyings thugasu chud pas na//'jig rten mgon zhes rab tu brjod//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//'dres pa med cing gnas pa med//
89b2(phal chen): sems can thams cad yid bzhin du//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu mthong //chos rnams thams cad mngon sangs rgyas//chos rnams kun la rab tu bzhugs//thams cad mkhyen pa thams cad gzigs//
89b3(phal chen): zhing rnams kun na rnam par mdzes//kha dog bsam gyis mi khyab par//sems can thams cad yid gzhin du//sems can mchog gi 'od rnams dang //de bzhin sku'i sprin kyang mthong //de bzhin gshegs 
89b4(phal chen): pa'i sku dag las//mi zad sangs rgyas sprul pa'i rnams//zhing rnams kun tu rab sgrog cing //chos kyi rgya mtsho shin tu ston//spu rnams re re kun nas kyang //phyogs bcur 'od zer dra ba dag/
89b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas gsung ni rab 'byin cing //gdul dka' thams cad rnam par 'dul//sangs rgyas 'od zer sgo rnams nas//sgra dbyangs zab mo rtag tu 'byung //sangs rgyas yon tan rgya 
89b6(phal chen): mtsho ni//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yulo//sangs rgyas chos kyi 'khor lo dag/mtha' dang dbung med rab tu sgor//ye shes zab mos shes bya ba//rgya chen phyogasu rab tu ston//'jig rten khams 
89b7(phal chen): rnams thams cad du//rnam par 'phrul ba gzhan dang gzhan//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun bkang zhing //yang dag sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//sku rnams re re kun las kyang //zhing rnams kun gyi rdul 
89b8(phal chen): mnyam pa//sangs rgyas sems can mnyam phyung zhing //rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu ston//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos rgya mtsho rnam par snang ba'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur 
90a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun khyab par//sangs rgyas sku ni rnam par mdzes//rgya chen gsum ni rab byung ba//
90a2(phal chen): zhing rnams rgya mtshor shin tu sgrog/'jig rten thams cad kun 'jug par//'jig rten mgon po shin tu mdzes//'gro ba kun gyi bsam mkhyen cing//byin gyi rlabs ni rab tu ston//sangs rgyas 'jig rten
90a3(phal chen): kun la mngon//sems can thams cad bsam pa bzhin//thams cad gzigs pas sangs rgyas kyi//rnam par 'phrul ba thams cad mthong//mtha' yas 'od mnga' sangs rgyas sprin//chos rnams
90a4(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i 'brug kyang sgrog/ye shes rnams rgyu'i tshul bzhin du//rgyal ba'i sras rnams de la 'jug/'jug pa rnams la 'byung ba med//'byung ba yod par rnam par bstan//gang la
90a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas bzhugs pa yi//chos rnams 'byung ba namakha' bzhin//'pho dang gnas pa mi mnga' ste//'on kyang rgyal ba kun na snang //sangs rgyas chos kyi dbyings las byung //
90a6(phal chen): de phyir sangs rgyas rab tu brjod//yul na bzhugs med rang bzhin med//de la 'byung ba mi mnga' ste//sku dang mtshan kyang de bzhin med//rgyal ba gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'o//'gro ba kun tu chos kyi
90a7(phal chen): sprin//bsam pa kun la rab tu byung//yang dag sangs rgyas rnams kun tu//sems can rgya mtsho 'dul ba mdzad//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na snang//'khor
90a8(phal chen): gyi rgya mtsho thams cad na//zhing rnams kun gyis rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku dag la//yon tan rgya mtsho zad mi shes//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas te//de dag yon tan zad pa
90b1(phal chen): med;;de nas byang chub sems dpa' spos 'od 'phro ba'i rgyan zla ba'i 'od kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//bsam gyis 
90b2(phal chen): mi khyab sangs rgyas sar//byang chub sems dpa' gang bzhugs pa//de dang de dag sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul thams cad mthong//ye shes lus kyis zhugs pa rnams//zhing kun rdul gyi sbubasu yang//
90b3(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang //bdag nyid lus kyang der mthong ngo//gzugs brnyan zhing rnams thams cad dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi spyan sngar 'byung//kun tu byin gyis rlabs rnams dang //
90b4(phal chen): rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu ston//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang //spyod pa gang gis rnam sbyangs pa//de dag zhing rnams thams cad du//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnam 'phrul mthong//
90b5(phal chen): kun tu mnyam pa nyid gyur cing//kun tu lus kyis rab gnas te//ye shes lus kyi rjes song nas//sangs rgyas spyod yul zhugs pa'o//sangs rgyas ye shes zhugs pa'i 
90b6(phal chen): rnams//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas kun gyi spu dag tu//rgya mtsho'i zhing la rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas kun gyi zhing dag tu//byin gyi rlabs rnams rab bstan pas//
90b7(phal chen): gzhan dang gzhan gyi lus dag dang //ming gis kun na rab tu gnas//byang chub snying por rnam 'phrul dang //byang chub mngon par 'tshang rgya zhing//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dag/skad cig sem+sa
90b8(phal chen): kyis ston pa byed//zhing rnams 'byam klas thams cad dang //bskal pa bye ba bsam yas dag/byang chub sems dpa' mnyam bzhag rnams//skad cig sems kyis rnam par ston//sangs rgyas 
91a1(phal chen): @#//zhing rnams thams cad na//byang chub sems dpa'i sprul pa rnams//sangs rgyas sku la rnam spyod kyang//mtha' ma rnyed par mi rungo//de nas byang chub sems dpa' seng ge'i 
91a2(phal chen): 'od zer snang bas mthu 'byin pa'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad kyi//chos 
91a3(phal chen): kyi 'khor lo rab tu 'khor//chos dbyings tshul rnams thams cad kyang//sangs rgyas sprin gyis mngon tu yog/'jig rten khams rnams rgya mtsho dang//phyogs bcu kun na gang yod pa//thams cad kun tu 
91a4(phal chen): 'khor lo 'khor//de ni smon lam rnam 'phrul ba'o//'khor rnamsa rgya mtsho rgya che ba//gzhan dang gzhan gyi ming dag gis//zhing rnams gnas pa kun tu yang//chos kyi 'khor lo kun 
91a5(phal chen): tu 'khor//rnam par snang mdzad byin gyi rlabs//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun tu yang//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dag/rjod cing zhing rnams kun tu 'byung//zhing kun rdul la 
91a6(phal chen): sku'i rnams//thams cad chos char rab tu 'bebs//'dres pa med cing 'byung ba med//'jig rten dag na rnam par mdzes//bskal pa bye ba grangs med par//zhing kun rdul phran sbubs dag tu//
91a7(phal chen): rnam par snang ba mdzad pa'i gsung//sngon gyi spyod pa rab tu sgrog/zhing rnams thams cad rdul snyed kyi//'od zer dra ba phyogs bcu na//sangs rgyas 'od zer kun sgo nas//byang chub mchog phyir sems 
91a8(phal chen): can 'dul//kun tu ma 'dres sku dag gis//chos dbyings yongasu rab gsal te//'gro rnams 'dul ba ji bzhin du//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu mthong//dus gsum zhing rnams thams cad kyi//
91b1(phal chen): mgon po ji snyed bzhugs pa rnams//de dag kun gyi mtshan kyang sgrog/sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul la ltos//da ltar byung dang 'das pa dang //sangs rgyas gang rnams ma byon pa//de dag kun gyi bsam yas 
91b2(phal chen): pa//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu grag/de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi yon tan gyi mdzod mi zad pa'i dpal gyi gzi brjid mar   mes sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par 
91b3(phal chen): bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//gang gis byang chub sems dpa' yi//ye shes tshogs 'di kun non cing //ye shes rtogs pa rnyed pa'i rnams//de dag 'di yi tshul la zhugs//
91b4(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam pa'i dbyangs//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu 'byung //sangs rgyas kun gyi spyod yul te//byang chub sems dpa'i ched du brjod//skad cig re re'i sems kun la//
91b5(phal chen): chos kyi dbyingasu rnam par rgyu//de bzhin nyid la gnas shing brten//chos rnams rgya mtsho kun la zhugs//rgyan gyi pharol phyin pa rnams//bskal pa bye ba bsam yasu//sangs 
91b6(phal chen): rgyas thams cad sku dag tu//zhing rnams kun na gnas shing spyod//rdul rnams re re'i stengs nas kyang //chos rnams kun ni rab tu 'byung //zhing rnams kun kyang khyab par byed//ye shes chags
91b7(phal chen): med spyod yulo//sangs rgyas thams cad spyod yul du//lus pa med par kun bkang zhing //rnam par 'phrul ba'i rnam spyod pas//sangs rgyas spyod yul rnams la zhugs//sangs rgyas kun gyi 
91b8(phal chen): rjes snyegs dbyangs//chos kyi dbyings ni rab sgrog pa//chos dbyings rjesu shes pa'i rnams//dbyangs kyi rgya mtsho rnams la spyod//bskal pa rgya mtshor ma 'dres par//sgra dang dbyangs 
92a1(phal chen): @#//rnams shin tu sgrog/dus rnams thams cad shes pas gsal//dbyangs kyis de dag sa la zhugs//sgra'i shes la rmongs pa med//sems can sgra yang spyod 
92a2(phal chen): yul te//thams cad mkhyen pa kun gyi yang//rnam par 'phrul ba'i dbyangs sheso//sa nas sar yang phyin gyur cing//stobs kyi sa la rab gnas te//bskal pa bye bar spyad pas na//chos nyid 'di la 
92a3(phal chen): rab tu 'jug/de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes kyi 'od zer shes rab kun nas sgron ma'i blo gros kyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu 
92a4(phal chen): bcad pa 'di dag smraso//gang la sangs rgyas bzhugs pa yi//chos rnams de dag mtshan nyid med//ye shes sa ni thob pa'i rnams//'jig rten gtso bo rab tu mthong//shes rab snang ba'i 
92a5(phal chen): kun 'od can//byang chub sems dpa' mnyam bzhag rnams//sangs rgyas thams cad kun gyi yang//sku yi rnam pa ra 'phrul la zhugs//chos kyi dbyings la rnam spyad cing//lus 
92a6(phal chen): kyi dngos la rab 'jug ste//zab mo'i chos rnams rtogs gyur nas//smon lam dag gis yang dag byung//bsod nams rgya mtshos byung gyur cing//ye shes sa la rnam par gnas//chos la rnam spyod 
92a7(phal chen): khong du chud//spyod pa'i mchog la rab tu spyod//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad kyi//sangs rgyas kun gyi drung dag tu//chos kyi dbyings la spyad pas na//rang bzhin lus ni rab tu mthong//zhing rnams 
92a8(phal chen): 'byam klas thams cad du//bskal pa bye ba bsam yasu//thams cad gzigs pa'i chos kyi rnams//rgya mtsho kun la rab tu spyod//bdag mthong bas ni lus dang rdul//gcig tu 'dres par 
92b1(phal chen): mngon par byas//ma skyes mtshan nyid med pa ste//zhing rnams kun tu'ang rnam par mdzes//'jig rten kun gyi bsam 'thun par//sems can kun la mngon sum du//lus kyi sprin ni bstan byas 
92b2(phal chen): shing||byang chub don tu sems can 'dul//sangs rgyas thams cad mthu   yis na//byang chub sems dpar brjod pa ste//gang gis mgon po mthong ba yi//rgyal ba'i byin rlabs shin tu zab//
92b3(phal chen): mgon po ji snyed gang bzhugs pa'i//byin rlabs mtha' dang dbung med de//chos dbyings rab tu gang byas nas//byang chub sems dpa' rnam 'tshang rgya//de nas byang chub sems dpa' me tog kun nas 'od
92b4(phal chen): 'phro gtsug pud rab tu 'phyang bas//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyisa brlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams kun tu 
92b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi//gsung gi dkyil 'khor sgra 'byin te//chos kyi dbyings rnams gang byas nas//yon tan sprin ni rab tu brjod//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//rnam 
92b6(phal chen): dag namakha' 'dra ba ste//gzugs kyi sku yang rab bstan cing//chos nyid 'di la shin tu 'jug/dga' dang mos pa'i shugs rnams dang //sangs rgyas stobs rnams thams cad la//rab tu 
92b7(phal chen): gnas shing de dag ni//de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes 'jug/shes rab chung ngus chos 'di dag/kun nas shes par mi nus te//ye shes mig ni rab sbyangs shing//'di'i tshul la 'jug par 
92b8(phal chen): 'gyur//rab tu gnas dang byin rlabs dang //chos kun rnam par spyad pa dang //rab tu 'jug dang ldan pa yi//mnyam pa nyid la rab tu zhugs//chos rnams mtha' yas glong gi rnams//chos kun 
93a1(phal chen): mtshan nyid rnams la te//kun mkhyen nyid la rab zhugs shing //chos kyi rgya mtsho kun la spyod//sangs rgyas zhing na kun 'khod cing //thams cad kun na rnam par mdzes//'ong 
93a2(phal chen): ba med cing 'phos pa med//sangs rgyas chos nyid de 'dra'o//gzugs brnyan 'dra ba'i sku rnams ni//sems can rgya mtsho'i nang na snang //mos pa ci 'dra yod pa'i rnams//de bzhin mgon po kun gyis mthong //
93a3(phal chen): kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang //spyod pa mang po sus sbyangs pa//de dag thams cad ba spu la'ang //rnam 'phrul gzhan dang gzhan dag mthong //lus kyi rnams ni re res kyang //chos kyi dbyings rnams 
93a4(phal chen): kun tu khyab//'khor lo kun na 'khor ba ste//chos kyi 'khor lo bsam mi khyab//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'od zer dang //gzi brjid mi zad pa'i rgyal po blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi 
93a5(phal chen): rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshi gsu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas sku ni thams cad nas//'od zer sprin mang phyogs rnams bcur//kha dog 
93a6(phal chen): mtha' yas 'bar ba'i 'od//sems can bsam pa kun la 'phro//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad kyi//snying po byang chub kun na bzhugs//bdud rnams thams cad rab btul te//'khor 
93a7(phal chen): gyi nang na rnam par mdzes//thams cad rdul na'ang ma 'dres par//'od zer dkyil 'khor rgyas par 'gengs//phyogs rnams gzhan dang gzhan lam tu//zhing gi rgyud rnams rab tu ston//zhing gi 
93a8(phal chen): rnams ni rgya mtsho'i grong //phyogs kyi rnams kyang kun tu gnon//zhing rnams thang chen mnyam zhing dag/in dra nI lar kun nas gnas//spyi'u tshugs dag dang snrel zhi dang //pad+mo'i dbyibs 
93b1(phal chen): dang khyad khyud dang //dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i khyad par du//gzugs rnams kun gyis rab tu gnas//chos kyi dbyings rnams gar phyin par//thug pa med par 'gram 'dal ba//de dag kun gyi 'khor rnamasu//
93b2(phal chen): shin tu bstan pa'i chos 'byung ngo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dbyings na//'khod pa'i zhing rnams bsam mi khyab//de dag kun tu chos nyid 'di//'jig rten mgon po rnams kyang ston//tha dad med pa'i chos kyi
93b3(phal chen): dbyings//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu 'khor//gcig gi tshul gyi rnam pa yis//chos bstan pa ni kun tu 'byung //sangs rgyas gsung gi dkyil 'khor ni//tshul rnams thams cad rab tu brjod//dad pa rgya
93b4(phal chen): mtsho'i tshul dag gis//mi zad tshul rnams rab tu bstan//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun na yang //rgyal ba rnams ni mig yor 'drar//snying po byang chub rnams na snang //'byung ba dag kyang 
93b5(phal chen): yod pa min//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyingasu 'od 'phro ba rnam par snang ba 'byung ba'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/
93b6(phal chen): de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//blta zhing blta zhing sangs rgyas rnams//sku gzugs bsam gyis mi khyab ste//dga' ba rgya mtsho kun kyang 'byung //chos kyi shugs ni rab tu 'phel//mtshan
93b7(phal chen): rab kun nas sangs rgyas rnams//phyogs bcu kun tu rab tu 'byung //rdul rnams kun tu 'jug pa yis//sangs rgyas sprin kyang de bzhin bstan//skad cig skad cig sems kun la//sangs rgyas 'od ni
93b8(phal chen): dpal gyis 'bar//rnam 'phrul gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na snang //byang chub sems dpa' shes rab che//rgya mtsho chos las 'byung ba rnams//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin rlabs
94a1(phal chen): @#//pa/'di'i yul la rnam par spyod//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam la//gang rnams shin tu gnas gyur pa//de dang de rnams zhing dag tu//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul kun la lta//
94a2(phal chen): ye shes zab mo rnam gnon cing //chos kyi dbyings la gzhol ba ni//mos pa rgya mtsho shes pa ste//smon lam kun gyis byung ba'o//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku dang gsung //chags pa med la rab tu zhugs 
94a3(phal chen): spyod pa gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//spyod yul kun kyang khong du chud//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gnas shing //ye shes spyod yul gyur pa'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings la rab brtson pa//rgyal ba rnams 
94a4(phal chen): kyi sa gnon to//skad cig sems kyis zhing gi rnams//sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul mnyam pa//de dag chos nyid ji bzhin du//rdul gcig steng du rnam par sbyong //zhing rnams ji snyed 
94a5(phal chen): thams cad na//zhing gi rnam 'phrul gang yod pa//chos nyid 'di la zhugs pa'i rnams//zhing gcig du ni sdud pa byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' stobs dang /brtson 'grus 
94a6(phal chen): chags pa med pa'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad du//de dag gsung 
94a7(phal chen): ni zab mo 'byung //dbyangs kyi yan lag kun rgyas pa//chos kyi sprin gyi char 'bebs so//brjod pa rgya mtsho thams cad kyis//sems can kun gyi skad dag tu//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun tu 
94a8(phal chen): yang //chos rnams bstan pa rab tu 'byung //zhing rnams kun tu ma lus par//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul mthong zhing //sangs rgyas gsung rnams thos pa dang //byang chub mchog phyir kun kyang 
94b1(phal chen): 'jug/rdul rnams re re'i steng nas kyang//chos kyi dbyings kyi phyogs tshul kyis//sangs rgyas zhing rnams kun bkang zhing//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul ston pa byed//chos kyi sku ni tha dad min//
94b2(phal chen): namakha' bzhin te chags pa med//gzugs kyi skur ni rnam dmigs pa//mig yor snang ba'i mtshan nyid do//mig yor yul la mi gnas te//namakha' bzhin du dngos po med//mnyam pa nyid 
94b3(phal chen): 'di rtogs gyur nas//ye shes rgya mtsho mthong bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas sku ni ma skyes te//gzung ba med cing byung ba med//namakha'i mnyam pa nyid dang mtshungs//gzung du med las 'di bstan to//
94b4(phal chen): zhing rnams kun gyi sangs rgyas rnams//de dag spu gcig rtse la tshogs//ye shes mig dang ldan pa yis//spu rnams kun tu rnam 'phrul mthong//rnam par snang mdzad smon lam ste//
94b5(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings ni kun bkang zhing//bla med chos kyi 'khor lo dag/zhing rnams kun na rtag tu 'khor//ji ste bskal pa bsam yas par//spu rtse gcig tu rnam sprul pa//sangs 
94b6(phal chen): rgyas kun gyis bshad mdzad kyang//de'i mtha' ni rnyed par dga'//ji ltar gling bzhi pa 'dir/de bzhin gshegs pas rnam par sprul pa'i snying po byang chub tu phyogs bcu nas/byang chub 
94b7(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams 'dus par gyur pa/de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i gling bzhi pa thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa re rer yang/de bzhin gshegs pas rna par sprul pa'i snying po bya nga chub dag
94b8(phal chen): tu/byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho byed pa'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun nas 'dus par gyur to//'di na ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khyams thams cad kyi snying
95a1(phal chen): @#//po byang chub/de bzhin gshegs pas rnam par sprul pa dag tu/kun nas 'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de dag kyang chos nyid 'di kho na rab tu ston to////
95a2(phal chen): shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/de bzhin gshegs pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////
95b1(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings su 'jug pas rgyas par 'gengs pa'i ye shes dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad yongasu grub par byed pa bskal pa thams cad du smon lam rgya mtshos rnam par snang bar 
95b2(phal chen): byed pa'i 'od can/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo ston cing rjod pa'i byin gyi rlabs la mnyam bar bzhag par gyur to//ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos/bcom 
95b3(phal chen): ldan 'das kyi zhabs kyi drung du byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa rnam bar snang mdzad kyi sku'i snying po zhes bya ba 'di la mnyam par bzhag pa de bzhin du/'jig rten 
95b4(phal chen): gyi khams 'byam klas pa/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/namakha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa/phyogs rgya mtsho thams cad kyi khamasu gtogs pa/dus gsum thams cad na yod pa'i sangs 
95b5(phal chen): rgyas kyi zhing 'byung ba'i khams chos kyi dbyings rgya chen pos non pa'i rigasu gtogs pa/shin tu phra zhing bsgribs pa med pa dang /phyogs rgya chen po rgya mtsho thams cad snang 
95b6(phal chen): ba 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i risu gtogs pas rnam par non pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyan gyis rnam par gzigs pa sangs rgyas kyi stobs thogs pa med pas chud pa/chos kyi dbyings 
95b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi sku thams cad kyi gzugs brnyan rab tu snang bas/chos kyi dbyings kyi rdul ma lus pa kun tu gtogs pa/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du rgyas par gang ba'i 'jig rten gyi 
95b8(phal chen): khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba dang mnyam pa dag tu rdul phra mo re re la'ang zhing 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs nas/zhing re re zhing yang de bzhin gshegs pa 
96a1(phal chen): @#//'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs te/de bzhin gshegs pa re re'i zhabs kyi drung du yang lus 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyedakyis/tinge 
96a2(phal chen): 'dzin sangs rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sku'i snying po zhes bya ba 'di la mnyam par bzhag go/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos byang chub sems dpa'i tinge 'dzin sangs
96a3(phal chen): rgyas thams cad de bzhin gshags pa rnam par snang mdzadakyi sku'i snying po zhes bya bde la mnyam par bzhag pa'i rjes la/de'i tshe phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad nas de bzhin gash+ye
96a4(phal chen): pa thams cad kyis zhal bstan to//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangas rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis 'di skad ces kyang bka' stsal te/rigs kyi bu khyod de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge
96a5(phal chen): 'dzin sangs rgyas thams can de bzhin gshegs pa rnams par snang mdzad kyi sku'i snang po 'di la mnyam bar gzhog pa legso legaso//kye rgyal ba'i bu khyod la phyogs bcu'i
96a6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa 'di dag thams cad kyang/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad 'di'i sngon gyi smon lam gyi byin gyis brlabs dang /khyod kyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi
96a7(phal chen): smon lam dang /spyod pas yongasu dag pa'i yang mthus 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho thams cad rnam par dbye ba dang /
96a8(phal chen): chos thams cad kyi tshul gyi phyogs rgya mtsho ma lus par snang bar bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba rnam par dag par bya ba dang /chags pa 
96b1(phal chen): med par 'jig rten gyi khams 'byam klas pa thams cad yongasu gnan pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi yul thams cad la chags pa med par 'jug par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi 'khor thams cad sgo 
96b2(phal chen): kun nas rab tu brjod pa dang /chos kyi tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la zhugs pas thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yongasu gzung ba dang /chos kyi snang ba thams cad kyi tshul rnam par blta ba dang /sems 
96b3(phal chen): can gyi dbang po rgya mtsho thams cad yongasu shes par bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsung gi yan lag rgya mtsho'i dbyangs yongasu gzung ba'i phyir byin gyis rlob pa mdzad do//de nas sangs rgyas 
96b4(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po la/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi stobs la rab tu 'jug pa'i ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrubaso//chos 
96b5(phal chen): kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbung med pa la rab tu 'jug pa'i ye shas kyang nye bar bsgrubaso/dus gsum kyi sangs rgyas kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad yang dag par 'byung ba'i ye shes kyang nye bar bsgru
96b6(phal chen): so//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad 'jig pa dang 'du ba'i ye shes kyang nye b.rabasgrubaso//chos kyi dbyings 'byam klas pa mtha' dang dbung med pa 'jug pa'i ye shes kyang nyes bar bsgrubaso//sangs 
96b7(phal chen): rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa zab mo tha mi dad pa dang /tinge 'dzin thams cad la rnam par gnas pa'i ye shes kyang nye b.rab bsgrubaso//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho la rab tu 'jug pa'i ye
96b8(phal chen): shes kyang nye bar bsgrubaso//sems can thams cad kyi skad rgya mtsho dang chos kyi 'khor lo'i nges pa'i tshig gi ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrubos//'jig rten gyi khams mang po thams cad kyi rjesu song zhing chos kyi dbyings
97a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad du lus yang dag par gzhol ba'i ye shes kyang nye bar bsgrubaso//sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi yan lag dang sgra dbyangs kyang nye bar bsgrubaso/ji ltar 'jig rten gyi khams 
97a2(phal chen): 'di'i bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs kyi drung na 'dug pa la nye bar bsgrubs pa de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag dang rdul shin tu phra ba'i khongs na lus 
97a3(phal chen): 'khod pa thams cad la yang nye bar bsgrubaso//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar ting nge 'dzin de nyid kyi chos nyid kyi chos nyid thob pa'i phyir ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis sprul pa'i sangs rgyas 
97a4(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das de dag gis phyag g.yas pa mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos rnam par mdzes par brgyan pa/'od zer gyi dra ba'i me tog sna tshogs shin tu rgyas pa 'od kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag 
97a5(phal chen): pa'i sprin 'gyed pa/rin po che 'od 'phro ba thams cad kyi sprin gyi sa rnam par gsal bar gyur pa/phyogs thams cad du spo sa 'od 'phro ba'i sprin gyi 'od kyis rnam par snang ba/sangs rgyas 
97a6(phal chen): thams cad kyi gsung gi yan lag gi dkyil 'khor gyi rjes su sgra 'byin pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba dang /khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba yang dag par ston pa/byang chub sems dpa' 'das pa dang /
97a7(phal chen): ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyi kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gyi dkyil 'khor rab tu snang ba rnam par ston pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i snang ba yang dag par ston pa /
97a8(phal chen): dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi gzugs brnyan 'byung ba rnams brkang ste/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyi bor bzhag go//'dir ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams 
97b1(phal chen): rgya mtsho thams cad dang rdul shin tu phra ba'i khongs na yod pa thams cad du yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs kyi drung dag tu byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i spyi bo la phyag rgya mtsho'i sprin rnams rab tu bzhago/
97b2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang po la sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis reg ma thag tu de nas de'i mod la ting 'dzin de las ldang ba'i sgo rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 
97b3(phal chen): dus gsum thams cad du sems kyi skad cig re re la ting nge 'dzin las ldang ba'i ye shes ma 'dres pa la mkhas pa dang /ting nge 'dzin las ldang ba'ibyin gyi rlabs kyi sgo chos kyi dbyings dang /dus gsum du 
97b4(phal chen): mnyam pa'i rdul thams cad kyi sgo dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba thams cad kyi sgo dang /sems can ma lus pa'i gnas thams cad dang mnyam zhing 
97b5(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi sems rgya mtsho la 'jug pa'i sgo dang /sems can thams cad kyi lus dang ming gi 'khor lo la sa ldang ba'i sgo dang /sems can phyogs mtshams thams cad 
97b6(phal chen): du chos kyi dbyings rnam par 'byed pa'i sgo dang /rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi sku'i sprin mtha' dang dbung med par 'byam klas pa'i sgo dang /chos kyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bstan 
97b7(phal chen): pa'i sgo dag nas langs par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po ting nge 'dzin las langs ma thag tu/de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' de dag kyang so so re re zhing ting nge 'dzin rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jag 
97b8(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la rab tu zhugs par gyur to//gzungs rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdu shin tu phra ba snyed la yang rab tu zhugs par gyur te//
98a1(phal chen): @#//chos kyi tshul rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la yang rab tu zhugs par gyur to//so so yang dag par rig pa'i tshul rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jigs rten gyi 
98a2(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shinatu phra ba snyed la yang rabatu zhugs par gyur to//spyod pa rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul zhin tu phra ba snyed la yang reg tu zhugs par gyur to/chos kyi dbyings 
98a3(phal chen): kyi phyogs su rnam par snang zhing de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes snang ba'i yon tan gyi snying po can rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtenagi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shing tu phra ba snyed la yang rab1tu zhugs par 
98a4(phal chen): gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs dang /ye shes ma 'dres pa'i tshul rgya mtsho'i sprina'ajig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba rnyed la yang rab tu zhugs bar gyur te//de 
98a5(phal chen): bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi ba spu'i rtse mo thams cad la zhing gzhan dang gzhan rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la yang rag tu zhugs par 
98a6(phal chen): gyur to/byang chub sems dpa' re re zhing dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'pho ba dang /'byung ba dang /byang chub tu rnam par 'tshang rgya ba dang /mya ngan las 'das pa bstan pa rgya mtsho'i sprin 'jig
98a7(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdum shin tu phra ba snyed la'ang rab tu zhugs par gyur to//'dir ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor 
98a8(phal chen): rgya mtsho rnams dang rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad kyi khongs na yod pa kun tu yang byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho rnams rab tu zhugs par gyur te/sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang byang chub sems dpa' kun tu 
98b1(phal chen): bzang po'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chos nyid rab tu thob pas phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad ma lus par bag langs tsam du g.yos te/rin po che thams cad kyis rab tu brgyan par gnas shing 
98b2(phal chen): chos rgya mtsho thams cad kyi sgra yang shin tu grags par gyur te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad du yang nor bu'i rgyal po bcu'i sprin gyi char mngon par bab par gyur to//bcu gang zhe na/
98b3(phal chen): 'di lta ste/nor bu'i rgyal po mtshan dang ldan pa'i gser dpal gyi rgyal mtshan gyi dpal gyi sprin char mngon par bab po//'di lta ste/nor bu'i rgyal pa 'od kyis rnam par gsal bar byed pa'i sprin char mngon
98b4(phal chen): par bab bo//'di lta ste/nor bu'i rkyal po phyogs bcur rin po che'i 'khor lo rab tu 'grems pa'i sprin gyi char mngon na par bab bo//'di lta ste nor bu'i rgyal po byang chub
98b5(phal chen): sems dpa'i gzugs brnyan rin po che'i snying po'i sprin gyi char mngon parab bo//'di lta ste/nor ba'i rgyal po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mtshan gyi 'khor lo rab tu sgrog pa'i sprin
98b6(phal chen): gyi char mngon par babo//'di lta ste/nor bu'i rgyalapo zhing rnam par snang bar byed cing snying po byang chub tu dpal rab tu 'bar ba'i sprin gyi char mngon parag bab bo//'di lta ste//nor bu'i rgyal po phyogs bcur snang
98b7(phal chen): ba'i sprul pa 'gyed pa'i sprin gyi char mngon pa bab po//'di lta ste nor bu'i rkyal po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi yon tan gyi 'khor lo dang mthu rab tu grag par byed pa'i sprin gyi char
98b8(phal chen): mngon par bab bo//'di ltaste/nor bu'i rgyal po nyi ma'i dpal gyi gzi brjid kyi sprin gyi char mngon par bab po//'di lta ste/nor bu'i rgyas po rol mo'i sgra yid du 'ong bas phyogs thams cad khyab
99a1(phal chen): @#//par byed pa'i sprin gyi char mngon par bab ste/nor bu'i rgyal po'i sprin gyi char bcu po 'di dag mngon par bab par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ba spu'i sgo thams cad dang /'od 
99a2(phal chen): zer gyi sgo thams cad nas kyang tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag byung bar gyur to//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun na kun tu bzang po de//pad+mo yid du 'ong la rab tu 'dug pa mthong //kun tu rnam 'phrul rnam pa 'di 'dra ston byed pa//
99a3(phal chen): ting 'dzin yul la 'jug pa mtha' dang dbung med do//lus rnams thams cad nas kyang kun tu bzang po de//sprul pa rab tu 'gyed cing chos dbyings rtag par 'gengs//ting 'dzin kun gyi tshul dang ming dang rnam 
99a4(phal chen): 'phrul ba//thams cad dbyangs kyis chags pa med pa rgya che sgrog/ting 'dzin thams cad gzhan dang gzhan gyi rnam 'phrul gyis//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad zhabs kyi drung dag tu//rgyal 
99a5(phal chen): ba kun gyi spyod yul yangs pa gang byas te//zhing rnams kun tu rnam par 'phrul pa mang po ston//zhing kun rgyal ba kun gyi zhabs drung ji bzhin du//de ltar zhing kun shin tu phra ba'i rdul phran 
99a6(phal chen): la//ting nge 'dzin rnam par 'phrul ba thams cad ma lus ston//rnam par snang mdzad smon lam dbyings la rab tu zhugs//kun tu bzang po'i lus ni nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//thams cad zhing la mi gnas 
99a7(phal chen): de bzhin nyid la gnas//lus kyi sprin ni 'gro ba kun dang mnyam par kun bkye zhing //sems can kun la bsam pa bzhin du de dag ston//sangs rgyas sku rnams kun la zhing rnams gang yod pa//
99a8(phal chen): mtha' dang dbung med kun la rab tu zhugs pa rnams//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gnas pa dag la bstan//rnam par 'phrul pa 'di 'dra dam pa tshad kyang med//'khor rnams rgya mtsho rdul bral mtha' 
99b1(phal chen): dang dbung med pa//tshad med thams cad lus kyi nang du kun chub cing //dbyangs la zhing mang dra bar rnam 'tshang rgya ba ston//skad cig sems la bskal pa mang po de bzhin ston//zhing rnams thams cad 'byam 
99b2(phal chen): klas pa na rnam gnas shing//rnam par 'phrul ba dam pa ston par rab tu snang //phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing gi dbyings rnams ma lus spyod//kun tu bzang po de la rnam lta kun kyang mthong//rgyal ba kun 
99b3(phal chen): gyi yon tan ye shes stobs la zhugs//ting 'dzin sa ni tshul rnams kun gyis rab tu mnon//chos rnams 'byam klas gzhan dang gzhan la rab tu zhugs//rang gi byang chub spyod pa sngan chad spyad pa 
99b4(phal chen): ston//de ltar zhing rnams kun tu bsam yas rnam 'phrul ba//phyogs rnams rgya che kun tu bkang ste rab tu bstan//ting 'dzin rnams 'jug pa rab tu bstan pa'i phyir//rgyal ba'i 
99b5(phal chen): 'od zer sprin las yon tan legs sgra 'byung //de nas byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor yod pa thams cad sangs rgyas kyi mthus thal mo sbyar te//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu 
99b6(phal chen): bzang po la lta zhing dbyangs kyi nga ro gcig tu de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'ing dag smraso//rgyal ba thams cad chos las skyes pa ste//smon lam kun gyis sangs rgyas rab tu byung //de bzhin nyid mnyam 
99b7(phal chen): namakha'i snying po ste//chos kyi sku yis rnam par rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams 'byam klas kun la bzhugs med kyang //kun tu bzang po'i 'khor rnams kun na snang //phyogs bcu kun tu 
99b8(phal chen): yon tan grags pa khyab//ye shes rgya mtsho rab tu snang ba mnga'//rgyal ba thams cad sku thob phyir spyod pa//phyogs bcu'i yon tan rgya mtsho yod pa rnams//rdul sbubs kun tu de dag chud 
100a1(phal chen): @#//byas shing //zhing rnams kun tu rnam par rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams kun gyi rgyal ba'i zhabs drung na//rgyal sras ting nge 'dzin dang spyod pa dang //chos rnams kun la spyod 
100a2(phal chen): cing gnas pa'i rnams//rdul dang mnyam pa'i bskal par kun kyang snang //chos kyi dbyings rnams lus kyis khyab byas shing //zhing rnams 'byam klas kun la shin tu zhugs//sems can rgya mtsho'i 
100a3(phal chen): don du de spyod cing //rdul phran kun la chos kyi dbyings su zhugs//chos kyi dbyings la de ltar zhugs nas kyang //rdul phran kun la mi zad tha mi dad//nam mkha'i dbyings ni lus kyis gang byas shing //rgyal 
100a4(phal chen): ba'i chos rnams yangs pa rab tu ston//yon tan rgya mtsho 'od kun gzhal du med//rnam yangs stobs chen 'od zer yon tan sprin//'gro ba rgya mtsho'i don du spyod byed cing //
100a5(phal chen): rgyal ba'i spyod lam gzhal du med pa ston//gang gi don du bskal pa rgya mtsho dag/mtha' yas par ni bzang po'i spyod spyad pa//de dag chos kyi dbyings rnams gang byas nas//thams cad 
100a6(phal chen): chos kyi sprin las rab tu brjod//zhing rnams ji ltar 'byung bar 'gyur ba dang //stobs bcu rnams kyang ji ltar 'byung 'gyur dang //sems can rgya mtsho ji ltar 'byung 'gyur ba//sems can rnams 
100a7(phal chen): la sgra dbyangs gtong zhing ston//'khor rnams yangs pa 'di dag thams cad kyang //kun tu bzang po khyod la mngon sum gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyis gnang bar gyur pa yi//chos kyi 'khor lo de ring 
100a8(phal chen): khyod kyis bskor////shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/kun tu bzang po'i ting nge 'dzin dang /rnam par 'phrul ba rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya ba ste/le'u 
100b1(phal chen): gsum pa'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad la rnam par bltas/sems can gyi kham+sa
100b2(phal chen): rgya mtsho la rnam par bltas//chos kyi dbyings rgya mtsho la rnam par bltas /sems can thams cad kyi las rgya mtsho la rnam par blta /sems can thams cadakyi bsam pa dang dbang po rgya mtsho la rnam
100b3(phal chen): par bltas/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi rgya mtsho la rnam par blta/dus rgya mtsho la rnam par ba ltas/sangs rgyas thams can kyi yang dag par 'byung ba'i smon lam rgya mtsho la rnam par blta'/
100b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba rgya mtsho la rnam par bltar nas/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras
100b5(phal chen): dag sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams thaMd 'du zhing 'jig pa yongasu dag pa 'byung ba'i ye shes ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'ad
100b6(phal chen): rnams kyi sems can thams cad kyi 'byung ba rgya mtsho mkhyen pa ni bsam gyis mi khyabo//sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul rgya mtsho thams cad la rnam par gnas pa'i ye
100b7(phal chen): shes ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rgya mtsho thams cad mtha' dang dbung med par bstan pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis ma khyabo/sang+yas bcom ldan
100b8(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sems can thamasacad kyi las rgya mtsho rnam par dgod pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyi mi khyab bo//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams kyis bsam pa dang dad pa dang /dbang po rgya mtsho la 'jug
101a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi thugs kyi skad cig gcig la/dus thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid thugs su chud pa'i ye shes ni 
101a2(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba rgya mtsho yang dag par bstan pa'i ye shes ni bsam gyis ma khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcam 
101a3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba'i ye shes ni bsam gyis me khyab bo//sangs rgyas btsom ldan 'das rnams kyi rnam par dgod pa rab tu bstan pa rgya mtsho ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//
101a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sku rab tu bstan pa yongs su dag pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo //sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi yon tan rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbung 
101a5(phal chen): med pas rnam par snang ba ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas sa bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su dag pa ni bsams gyis mi khyab bo//sangs d+yas bcom 
101a6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor yongs su dag pa mtha' yas pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi 'od zer rgya mtsho kha dog sna tshogs kyi sprin rab tu 'gyed pa mdzad pa ni/
101a7(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi sangs rgyas kyi dpal rin po che'i 'od 'phro ba rgya mtsho rnam par rgyu ba ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa//sangs rgyas bcom ldan
101a8(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung dang dbyangs rgya mtsho rab tu 'grub pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan
101b1(phal chen): smin par mdzad pa dang /gdul ba mdzad pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi gdul ba rgya mtsho thams cad la brtson pa don yod pa ni bsam gyis
101b2(phal chen): mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sa la rab tu bzhugs pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la
101b3(phal chen): 'jug pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab po//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi byin gyi rlabs kyi mthu ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo //sangs rgyas bcom ldan
101b4(phal chen): yul rnam par rgyu ba ni bsam gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan dangs rnams kyi stobs la thub pa med pa ni bsam gyis mi khya ba bo//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi mi 
101b5(phal chen): bsnyengs pa zil gyis mi non pa ni bsam gyis mi khyababo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'i 'das rnams kyi sangs rgya kyi tinge 'dzin la tha mi dad par spyod pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab//sangs rgyas
101b6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin bshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul ba ni bsam gyis mi khyabo//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis mnga' chen po'i ye shes rnam par dag pa ni bsam
101b7(phal chen): gyis mi khyab bo//sangs rgyas bcom ldang 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mi 'phrogs pa ni bsam gyis mi khyab ste/de dag thams cad sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs
101b8(phal chen): kyis byin du rlabs pas/sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyis ye shes rgya mtsho la gzud pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la rab tu 'god pa dang /
102a1(phal chen): @#//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad kyis rnam par brgyan pa dang /bskal pa rgya mtsho thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung 
102a2(phal chen): rjes su bsab pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho thams cad du chos kyi rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor rab tu brjod pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi dad pa rgya mtsho tha dad pa ji lta ba bzhin du rab tu bstan pa 
102a3(phal chen): dang /sems can thams can kyi dbang po rgya mtsho la sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi tshul kun tu snang ba yang dag par bskyed pa dang /sems can tha mas cad kyi bsam pa rgya mtsho'i sgrib pa'i ri thams cad rnam par 
102a4(phal chen): gzhom pa dang /sems can tham sa cad kyi sems dang spyod pa rgya mtsho la 'byung ba'i lam rnam par sbyang ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho thams cad byang chub sems dpa'i smon 
102a5(phal chen): lam rgya mtsho la rab tu dgod pa'i phyir yang dag par bstan par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho rnams de bas kyang shin tu 
102a6(phal chen): dga' bar bya ba dang /chos la dga' ba'i shugs thams cad rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /mos pa'i tshul yangs pa rgya mtsho yang dag par 'byung bar bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin dang /
102a7(phal chen): snying po sgo kun dang ldan pa rnam par dag par bya ba dang /kun tu bzang po'i smon lam rgya mtsho la rab tu dgos par bya ba dang /ye shes kyi mig dus gsum gyi rjes su song ba yongs su sbyang ba dang /'jig rten 
102a8(phal chen): thams cad kun nas snang bar byed pa'i snying po shes rab chen po'i rgya mtsho rnam par 'phel bar bya dang /chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad yongs su gzung ba'i gzungs kyi stobs yang dag par bskyed pa dang /
102b1(phal chen): snying po byang chub thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi yul thams cad rab tu brjod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi tshul snang ba'i 'od chos kyi dbyings rgya chen po dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la dga' 
102b2(phal chen): ba'i shugs rgya mtsho rnam par 'phel bar bya ba'i phyir /de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//yon tan rgya mtsho rnams kyi ye shes zab//mtha' dang dbung med des ni zhing rnams khyab//sems can 
102b3(phal chen): bsam la sbyor ba ji bzhin snang //rnam par snang mdzad chos kyi 'khor lo'o//bsam gyis mi khyab rgya mtsho 'i zhing yod pa//bskal pa rgya mtshor rgyal bas rnam sbyangs pas//sems can rgya mtsho 
102b4(phal chen): yongs su smin bya'i phyir//sems can nang na rgyal ba rnam par mdzes//sems can rgya mtsho 'byung ba bsam mi khyab//sangs rgyas yul la 'jug pa shin tu zab//srid pa'i 
102b5(phal chen): gnas can dad pa chung ba yis//sangs rgyas byang chub shes par mi nuso//gzhan du gang rnams shin tu sems brtan cing //dge ba'i bshes kyis yongasu bzung ba dang /
102b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas kun gyi byin gyis brlabs pa dag/sangs rgyas ye shes 'di la rab tu 'jug/gang rnams sems dag g.yo med sgyu med cing //snying rje'i bsam can khong nas byams dga' dang //
102b7(phal chen): dad pa che zhing rlabs chen de dag ni//'di dag thos shing dga' ba thob par 'gyur//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam sa dag dang //chos dbyings namakha'i dbyingasu khyab pa la//gnas shing spyod 
102b8(phal chen): pa rab tu sus spyod pa//de dag rgyal ba'i spyod yul 'di sheso//gang gis rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul 'di mthong ba'i//byang chub sems dpas thob bya legs par thob//gzhan gyi yul gyis 
103a1(phal chen): @#//shes par mi nus te//'di ni kun tu bzang pos rtogs pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis byin rlabs shing //'gro ba mtha' dang dbung med 'di dag na//kun tu
