103a2(phal chen): chos kyi 'khor lo rab 'khor ba//rnam par snang mdzad yul dang mthu yin no//bdag gi lus su zhing rnams thams cad kyang //rab tu zhugs shing de zhugs zhing rnams kun//de dag spu rtse kun 
103a3(phal chen): tu blta bar gyis//rgyal ba'i spyod yul shin tu bshad par bya//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam spyod pa rnams//mtha' dang dbung med mngon par sgrub pa dang //kun tu mig gi yul dang lus ldan 
103a4(phal chen): pa//de dag mgon po'i spyod yul 'di nyan to//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa ste//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa 
103a5(phal chen): dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams yongs su 'byung ba'i tshogs kyi rgyu 
103a6(phal chen): rkyen shin tu bstan pa dang /'di lta ste/'jig rten gi khams rgya mtsho gnas pa shin tu bstan pa dang /'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi 
103a7(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho'i lhun shin tu bstan pa dang 'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi gnas rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa dang 'id lta ste/'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho'i dbyibs dang rnam pa shin tu bstan pa dang/'di lta ste/'jig rten kyi 
103a8(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho'i lhun shing du bstan pa dang /'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa dang /
103b1(phal chen): mtha' yas pa'i rgyud rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa dang 'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa shin tu bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho bcu po 'di dag la sogs pa 
103b2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho shin tu bstan pa/'jig rten gyi khams rgya  mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams kyis gsungs so//
103b3(phal chen): gsung bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshothams cad ni rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs pa'i sgo//'jig rten gyi khams rgyam tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snying phrag 
103b4(phal chen): bcus byung ngo //'byung ngo //'byung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis rlabs dang chos nyid rab tu thob pa dang /sems can rnams 
103b5(phal chen): kyi las kyi tshogs dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yang dag par sgrub pa'i chos nyid dang /sems can rnams kyis kyang byang chub sems dpa' dag dang /
103b6(phal chen): bsod nams kyi rtsa ba skal pa 'thun pa bsags pa dang /byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi zhing yongs su dag pa'i smon lam gyi khyad par dang /byang chub sems rnams kyi spyod pas rnam par bsgrubs 
103b7(phal chen): pa'i smon lam gyis mngon par bsgrubs pa dang /byang chub sem dpa' rnams kyi mos pa'i dbang rnam par dag pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba 'jig rten las 'das pa dang /
103b8(phal chen): rgyu 'thun pas mngon par 'tshang rgya zhing sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa khyu mchog gi mthu dbyung ba dang /byang chub sems dad pa kun tu bzang po rnams kyi smon lam gyis smon par 'phrul ba'i sgo'o//kye 
104a1(phal chen): @#//rgyal ba'i sras dag/de ltar rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs pa'i sgo bcu po 'di dag la sogs pa rgyu dang rkyen la dmigs pa/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis 'jig 
104a2(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad byung ngo //'byung ngo //'byung bar 'gyuro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa 'di nyid gsal bar brjod pa'i phyir/
104a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas ye shes yul ni bsam yas pas//byin rlabs rnam 'phrul de dag 'di lta bu//zhing rnams 
104a4(phal chen): rgya mtsho bstan pa mtha' dbung med//rnam par snang mdzad chos kyis yongasu sbyangs//byang chub sems dpa' kunagyi ba sam pa bzhin//smon lam rgya mtsho bsam yas rnam sbyangs 
104a5(phal chen): shing //sems can las rnams rgya mtsho bsam yas pas//phyogs kun zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad 'byung //byang chub sems dpa'-i kun gyi rnam 'phrul dang //thams cad mkhyen pa nyid la 
104a6(phal chen): 'jug pa'i sgos//smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad mngon bsgrubs shing //namakha'i dbyings la 'byam klad si zhing 'byungo//spyod pa rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbung med rnams//spyod cing tshad med bde gshegs yul 
104a7(phal chen): la 'jug/phyogs kyi zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun sbyong zhing //bskal pa mtha' yas zhing rnams re re spyod//sems can bsam dbyings bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i//las kyis zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad byung //
104b1(phal chen): nyon mongs las kyis de dag nyon mongs bcas//mos dang bsam bas rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs//zhing rnams rgya mtsho rnam dag rgyan can rnams//rin chen rgyus byung sna tshogs dri med pa//dad pa rgya
104b2(phal chen): mtsho rnam par spel ba'i phyir//rgyal sras rnams ni phyogs bcur zhing rnams sbyong//chos dbyings rdul gyi khongasu rgyal sras rnams//kun tu bzang po'i spyad pa spyod pa dag/rdul gyi khongasu mtha' yas zhing
104b3(phal chen): ston te//rnam dag mkha' bzhin mtha' dang dbung yang med//namakha'i dbyings 'dra'i rnam 'phrul 'di 'dra ste//snying po byang chub rgyal ba'i zhabs drung na//pad+mo'i bstan 'khod kha dog mtha' yas kun//
104b4(phal chen): de dag zhing bcas lus kyi nang na 'khod//skad cig sems kyis dus gsum byung ba yi//zhing 'byung tshul dang 'jug pa kun kyang ston//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dus gsum zhing stan pa//
104b5(phal chen): rnam par snang mdzad rgya mtsho'i zhing mi zad//tshul gyi khyad par mtha' yas sna tshogs pas//rdul rnams kun la mtha' yas rgyan gyi rnams//sangs rgyas skad cig gcig la byin
104b6(phal chen): gyis rlob//rgyal gyi rnam pa tshul kun rab tu mkhyen//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la gnas pa shin tu bstan pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re re zhing yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu
104b7(phal chen): phra ba snyed de//'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho la la ni//rgyan thams cad kyi rgya mtsho kun la rab tu gnaso//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho la la ni namakha'i dbyings kyi gnas la rab tu gnaso//
104b8(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshe la la ni rin po che thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi rgya mtsho la rab tu gnaso//'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho la rab tu
105a1(phal chen): @#//gnaso//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog gi mdog la rab tu gnaso//'jig rten gyi kham sa rgya mtsho la la ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 
105a2(phal chen): dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor la rab tu gnaso//'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni sgyu ma lta bu'i las kyis byung ba/lha ma yin dang mthu bo che lta bu'i lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho la rab tu gnaso//'jigarten gyi khams 
105a3(phal chen): rgya mtsho la la ni 'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi lus kyi gzugs brnyan rgya mtsho la rab tu gnaso //'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho la la ni byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi lus rgya mtsho la rab tu 
105a4(phal chen): gnaso//'jig rten gyi khamams rgya mtsho la la ni byang chub ses dpa' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gyis byung ba'i rgyan rnam pa thams cad dang ldan pa'i rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas te/de dag la sogs 
105a5(phal chen): pa 'jig rten gyi khams rab tu gnas pa shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re re zhing yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub 
105a6(phal chen): sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho rab tu gnas pa shin du bstan pa'i don 'di nyid yongasu bstan pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi mthus/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//
105a7(phal chen): kun kyang zhing rnams rgya mtsho 'di dag rin chen rang bzhin dri med pa//rnam dag sna tshogs nor bu kun 'bar 'od zer rgya mtsho'i 'od ldan ltos//'di dag rgyal ba'i byin rlab rgyu yis dbyings legs 
105a8(phal chen): 'khor bar rnam par snang //de dag gis ni phyogs bcu'i namakha'i dbyings rnams gzhi kun rab tu khyab//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la la dri med 'od can namakha'i dbyings la gnas//snang ba'i snying po 'od 
105b1(phal chen): can nor bu'i rgya mtsho dag la legs par gnas//'gro ba de dbus rgyal ba 'khor la chos kyi 'khor lo bzang po skor//'di ni sangs rgyas yul yin sems can rgya chen shin tu mgu byed pa'o//la la 
105b2(phal chen): me tog sgron ma kun tu rgyas pa'i mu tig nor bus brgyan//'od zer dra ba'i kun nas sprin 'bar spos 'phro sprin gyi 'od can te//zhing mang mtha' dang dbung med rgya chen pad+mo'i rgya mtsho dag la gnas//
105b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas rnams kyis rab brgyan zhing rnams 'byam klas kun kyang rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams rgya mtsho dkyil 'khor dbyings rnams la la sangs rgyas byin la gnas//yangs shing rin chen mang 
105b4(phal chen): ldan rgya che de dag byang chub sems dpas gang //lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho mang tshogs lha dbang lus la de bzhin gnas//de na bcom ldan rnam par snang ba mdzad kyi 'khor lo rtag tu 'khor//
105b5(phal chen): nor bu'i shing dang spos kyi 'phro'i sprin rnams mnyam pa'i dbyings la gnas//chu'i phung po dag dang rdo rje rgya mtsho brtan la'ang de bzhin gnas//la la gos dang brag dang rgyal mtshan 
105b6(phal chen): me tog sna tshogs rgya mtsho dag la gnas/kun na'ang rnam 'phrul 'di dag rgya chen rnam par snang mdzad ston pa mdzad//zhing rnams ring dang thung dang glong gi gzugs dang rnam pa sna tshogs te//sna 
105b7(phal chen): tshogs rgyan can snying po dri ma bral ba yongs dag dri med snang //rnam pa sna tshogs de dag smon lam rgya mtsho'i dbyings la rab tu gnas//sangs rgyas sprin gyis kun nas yog par rtag tu nam mkha'i 
105b8(phal chen): dbyings la gnas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho 'byam klas la la spyi'u tshugs yod dang med la gnas//byang chub sems dpa'i cod pan rin chen 'khor los bzung bas dag par gnas//de nas sangs rgyas 
106a1(phal chen): @#//rnam 'phrul rgya chen thams cad kun nas rab tu snang rin chen sangs rgyas gsung rnams kun tu gtong ldan las kyis de dag sprul//chos kyi dbyings mnyam zhing rnams 'byam 
106a2(phal chen): klas mig yor sgyu ma dang yang 'dra//mig 'phrul rnam phye rnam dag dri med sems kyis shin tu bskyed pa ste//nam mkha'i dbyings rnams sna tshogs rgyan gyi snying po gnas 'gyur 'di la snang //las kyi 
106a3(phal chen): yul ni bsam gyis mi khyab 'di dag bde bar gshegs pas bstan//skad cig sems kyis zhing rnams 'byam klas 'gro ba kun dang mnyam pa'i rnams//zhing rdul kun la kun tu bzang po rtag par 
106a4(phal chen): rab tu sgrub byed cing //de na bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba smin pa ra bya phyir spyad pa spyod//chos dbyings rgyas par bkang ste de dag kun tu rnam 'phrul rgya che ston//rdul rnams kun na chos 
106a5(phal chen): dbyings yangs pa'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun kyang gnas//sangs rgyas sprin gyis byin gyis brlabs pas kun kyang yog cing yongs su rgyas//ji ltar rdul phran gcig la rnam sprul de 
106a6(phal chen): bzhin 'gro ba kun na'o//rnam par snang ba'i sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul rgya chen rab tu ston pa mdzad//zhing rnams 'byam klas thams cad mig yor lta bu sgyu ma smig rgyu 'dra//snang ba med cing 'byung 
106a7(phal chen): med phyogs bcu dag na 'gro dang 'ong ba med//mkha' la gnas pa de dag rtag par byung zhing 'jig par de bzhin 'gyur//shin tu yangs pa de dag smon lam rnam sbyangs las kyis yongs su bzung //
106a8(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho rnams kyi dbyings ni rnam pa sna tshogs te/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho 'byam klas pa la la ni kun nas zlum po'o//'jig rten gyi khams 
106b1(phal chen): rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la ni kun nas gru bzhi'o//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la ni nam mkha'i rnam pa du mar rnam par phye ba'o//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa
106b2(phal chen): la la ni kun nas 'khyil pa'i dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la ni 'od 'phro ba'i ri'i dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho 'byam klas pa la la
106b3(phal chen): ni shing sna tshogs kyi lhun gyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la la ni me tog sna tshogs kyi dbyibasu 'dug go/'jig rten gyis khams rgya mtsho 'byam klasapa la
106b4(phal chen): la ni khyim sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'jigarten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'bya ma klas pa la la la ni sems can sna tshogs kyi lus kyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgya
106b5(phal chen): mtsho 'byam klas pa la la la na mtshan sna tshogs kyi dbyibasu 'dug ste//de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams kyi dbyibs shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya
106b6(phal chen): ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i dbyibs kyi tshul rgya mtsho 'di nyid yongasu bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas
106b7(phal chen): kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//rnam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho'i zhing la ltos//rnam phye sna tshogs mtha' yas dbyibs
106b8(phal chen): su gnas//dbyibs kyis rnam par phye ba sna tshogs te//phyogs rnams kun na zhing rnams rgya mtsho snang//la la zlum po la la zur gsum ste//de dag la la phyogs na gru bzhir gnas//
107a1(phal chen): @#//nor bu padamo 'khor lo yan lag brgyad//kun kyang las rnams rgya mtshos rnam par bris//rdul med 'od kyi dbyibs dang tshul 'dzin cing//gser las grub pa 
107a2(phal chen): rnam phye sna tshogs te//sgo mang mtha' yas pa nas rgyu zhing 'jug/las rnams rgya mtsho bsam pa dag pas gnas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho sna tshogs snying po can//nam mkha' 'di la ji ltar sprin 
107a3(phal chen): gnas bzhin//de dag rin chen mang gzhi rnam par phye//de dag kun na sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes//zhing rnams 'di kun sems kyis rnam par brtags//sna tshogs rgyan dang snang bas kun na 
107a4(phal chen): gnas//rnam par 'phrul ba gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//bde dang sdug bsngal myong bar gyur pa ni//rnam par dag dang nyon mongs
107a5(phal chen): tshul bzhin te//chos nyid 'di ni rtag tu yod pa yin//de lta ra las rnams rgya mtsho bsam mi khyab//skra gcig rtse la ba sam gyis mi khyab zhing//thams cad rdul snyed sna tshogs 
107a6(phal chen): dbyibs su gnas//kun na sangs rgyas rnam parasnang mdzad bzhugs//'khor rnams rgya mtsho chos rnams rab tu ston//rdul gcig rtse la phra dang yangs pa'i rnams//thams cad rdul snyed kha 
107a7(phal chen): dog dbyibs rnams kun kyang rnam par brtags//de dag kun na rgyal ba'i 'khor lo 'khor//rdul rnams kun la rab tu zhing bstan pa//de dag smon lam rnam par sprul pa yin//nam mkha'i 
107a8(phal chen): dbyings su las kyi sprul pa ni//sna tshogs sems dang bsam pa rnam par brtags//sems can kun mnyam rdul rnams thams cad la//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul ba sprul pa dag/rnam 
107a9(phal chen): par snang mdzad chos nyid de yin te//rdul phran kun la zhing rnams shin tu gnas//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i lhun ngag ni gzhan dang gzhan du shes par bya ste//'di ltar 'jig rten 
107b1(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po che'i rgyan thams cad kyi lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni/rin po che gcig las rnam pa sna tshogs su bkod pa'i rgyan gyi lhun can
107b2(phal chen): no//'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho la la ni rin po che'-i rgyan thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi lhun can no//'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni 'od kha dog sna tshogs kyi lhun can no//
107b3(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho la la ni rgyan thams cad kyi mdog gi lhun can no//'jig rtenagy-i khams rgya mtsho la la ni rdo rje'i gzhi mi shigs pa'i lhun can no//'jig rten྾gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni
107b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul dang /byin gyi brlabs kyi lhun can no//'jig rjen gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni rin po che'-i mtshan nyid kyi lhun cano//'jig rten gyi khams rkya mtsho la la ni sang+sa
107b5(phal chen): rgyas kyis sprul pa'i lhun can no//'jigs rten gyi khams rgya mtsho las la ni nyi ma'i nor bu'i dkyil 'khor gyi lhun can no//'jigs rtan gyi khams rgya mtsho la la nirin po che shin
107b6(phal chen): tu phra ba 'dzin pa'i lhun can no//'jigarten gyi khams rgya mtsho la lani snang ba'i lhun cano//'jig rtenagyi khams rkya mtsho la la nirin po che thams cad kyi kha dog 'od 'phro ba'i rnam pa'i lhun
107b7(phal chen): can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la na ni dri dang kha dog sna tshogs kyi spos kyi lhun can no//'ji rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la na ni rin po che thams cad kyi me tog gi cod pan gyi lhun can
107b8(phal chen): no//'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho la ni rin po che thams cadakyi 'od kyi lhun can no//'jig rten gyi gnas rgya mtsho la la ni rgyan thams cad shin tu bstan pa'i lhun can no//'jag rten
108a1(phal chen): @#//gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni sems kyi rnam par rtog pa skad cig gis dmigs pa thams cad yongasu bstan pa'i lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la nibyang chub 
108a2(phal chen): sems dpa'i lus kyi rin po che'i dbyings kyi lhun can no//'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la nirin po che thams cad kyi me tog gi ze ba'i lhun can no//'jig rted gyi khams rgya mtsho la la nisangs 
108a3(phal chen): rgyas thams cad kyi gsung dbyangs rnam par dmigs pa'i lhun can no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos//yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i lhun shin tu bstan pa rgya mtsho 'di 
108a4(phal chen): la rab tu gzung bar bya ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la la ni//
108a5(phal chen): rin chen rang bzhin kun nas gnas//rdo rje bzhin du mi shigs sra//rin chen pad+mo rnams la gnas//la la 'od zer rang bzhin te//dag cing gzud med 'od zer 'byung//rgyan rnams 
108a6(phal chen): kun gyi 'od zer gyis//sna tshogs mkha' bzhin dri ma med//la la 'od kyi rang bzhin te//dag cig 'od gsal zer la gnas//'od kyi sprin can sna tshogs 'od//byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
108a7(phal chen): kyi gnas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la ni//mig yor lta bur gnas pa ste//brjod du med cing gzung du med//smon lam dbang gis rab tu byung//la la nor bu mu tig rgyu//snying po thams cad rnaMr 
108a8(phal chen): 'bar//mu tig 'khor lo'i gzhi dag pa//byang chub sems dpas yongasu gang//rin chen 'od 'phro byung ba'i zhing//'od 'phro'i sprin gyis mngon khebs shing//rin chen thams cad sna tshogs ldan//
108b1(phal chen): thams cad las kyis sprul pa yin//la la mtshan las byung ba ste//gzhi kun mtshan gyis kun tu bris//cod pan rnam pa sna tshogs bkod//sangs rgyas kun gyis mngon par sprul//rgya
108b2(phal chen): mtsho'i sems las byung ba yi//zhing rnams mos pa nyid la gnas//yul na mi gnas sgyu ma gzhin//yongasu rtog pas yang dag bskyed//sangs rgyas sku'i 'od zer gyi//nor bus sna tshogs kun
108b3(phal chen): tu gnas//sangs rgyas sprin rnams rnam par snang//sangs rgyas kun gyis rab tu sprul//la la kun tu bzang po yis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho shin tu sprul//nam mkha'i rgyan rnams thams cad dang //
108b4(phal chen): smon lam rnams kyis rnam par bris//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed
108b5(phal chen): du shes par bya ste/'di ltar dmigs pa rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi sprin rab tu 'phros pa'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi yon tan gyi 'khor
108b6(phal chen): lo rjesu brjod cing rab tu 'byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi las rnam par smin pa rgya mtsho las rab tu byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di
108b7(phal chen): ste|byang chub sems dpa' tham sa cad kyi smon lam rgya mtsho'i glong yongasu ston pa'i rgyan dang /'di ltar ste/dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gzugs brnyan sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa'i
108b8(phal chen): rgyan dang /'di lta ste/sems kyi skad cig la bskal pa mtha' yas par rnam par 'phrul ba'i yul yongasu ston pa'i rgyan dang/'di lta ste/rgyan gyi dmigs pa thams cad las sangs rgyas kyi sku rab
109a1(phal chen): @#//tu 'byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste/rin po che dang /spos thams cad kyi sprin rab tu 'byung ba'i rgyan dang 'di lta ste/snying po byang chub kyi rgyan dang /'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par 
109a2(phal chen): ston pa'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste /byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang /spyod pa yongs su ston pa'i rgyan dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i 
109a3(phal chen): rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa 'di nyid yongs 
109a4(phal chen): su bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i rgya che mtha' yas te//rnam dag 
109a5(phal chen): las kyi rnam pa sna tshogs gnas//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnam sa rgya mtsho rnams kun kyang //sna tshogs rgyan can yang sa par kun nas gnas//kha dog sna tshogs yangs pa'i zhing gi 
109a6(phal chen): rnams//rgya mtsho rtag par phyogs rnams kun tu 'byung //rgyan dang rin chen 'od 'phro sna tshogs sprin//sgra 'byin zhing gi dbyings rnams khebs par byed//byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho 
109a7(phal chen): rnams//de dag kun kyang rgya mtsho'i zhing na grag/sna tshogs rgyan dang smon lam sprin gyi rnams//de dag zhing mang rgya mtshor sgra yang 'byin//'gro ba'i las rnams rgya mtsho yangs pa dag/rgyan gyi sprin 
109a8(phal chen): rnams kun nas sgrar 'byin te//sems can de dag rnam smin ji 'dra ba//sangs rgyas byin gyis kun nas sgra yang sgrog/dus gsum 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa yi//rnam 'phrul zhing rnams 
109b1(phal chen): rgya mtsho kun na snang//dmigs pa re re la yang sangs rgyas kun//de 'dra'i zhing mang rgya mtsho'i rgyan la ltos//'das pa'i bskal pa de bzhin ma 'ongs dang //phyogs bcu'i da ltar byung ba ji
109b2(phal chen): snyed pa'i//bskal pa de dag rgyan mang yangs pa yang//zhing rnams rgya mtsho re re dag na snang//dmigs pa kun la sangs rgyas sprin gyi rnams//'gro dang mnyam pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gengs//
109b3(phal chen): rnam par 'phrul zhing sems can rnam par 'dul//'di ni zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i rnam thar to//spos 'ong sprin dang de bzhin me tog klong//rgyan rnams sna tshogs thams cad kun nas kyang //
109b4(phal chen): rtag par kun tu khyab par rab tu 'byung//'di ni zhing rnams sa rgya mtsho'i rnam thar to//phyogs bcu kun gyi snying po bya nga chub rnams//sna tshogs rgyan can kun kyang ma lus par//
109b5(phal chen): thams cad rgya mtsho'i zhing 'di dbyings dag na//nam kha'i sprin bzhin rnam par ston pa snang //rgyal sras kun tu bzang po thams cad kyi//zhing rnams mtha'
109b6(phal chen): yas 'byam klas rgyan gyi rnams//'gro ba'i grangs snyed bskal pa rgya mtshor rnam sbyangs pa//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun nas rab tu snang //kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/'jig rten gyi
109b7(phal chen): khams rgya mtsho yongasu dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge ba'i bshes gnyen
109b8(phal chen): thams cad la brtan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa bas skal pa mnyam pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi chos kyi dbyings kyi bsod nams rgya chen po'i sprin rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /byang chub sems
110a1(phal chen): @#//dpa' rnams kyi mos pa'i dkyil 'khor rgya chen po rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rab tu brtan pa'i yul la rnam par 
110a2(phal chen): spyod pa dang /byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho thams cad yongasu rdzogs par bya ba dang /yongs su sbyang ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi byang chub semasadapa'i sa thams 
110a3(phal chen): cad kyi dbyings la rab tu dgod cing gzud pa'i rnam par spyod pa dang /byang chub ses dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa dang /'byung ba rgya mtsho la rnam par spyod pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams 
110a4(phal chen): cad mngon par bsgrub pa rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems/dpa' rnams kyi rgya mtsho thams cad mngon par bsgrub pa dang /dag pa la rab tu 'jug pa dang /'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa'
110a5(phal chen): rnams kyi stobs kyi tshul yang dag par bsgrubs pa rnam par dag pa dang/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho yongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams
110a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho yongasu dag par bya ba'i tshul rgyamatsho 'di nyid rnam par bstan pa'i
110a7(phal chen): phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad sna tshogs rgyan//smon lam tshul rnams rgya mtsho 
110a8(phal chen): kun gyis byung //kha dog mtha' yas las kyi rgya mtshos dag/zhing rnams rgya mtsho rtag tu 'od mang 'gyed//sngan chad dge ba'i bshes gnyen bsnyen bkur cing //skal pa mnyam pa'i las dge yongs sbyangs
110b1(phal chen): te//snying rje byams pas 'gro yangs kun bkang zhing //zhing rnams rgya mtsho yangs pa 'di kun sbyangs//chos sgo kun tu ting 'dzin sa kun gyis//sangs rgyas yon tan rgya mtsho yongs sbyangs te//bsam gtan tshul
110b2(phal chen): dang rnam thar sa sbyang zhing //zhing rnams rgya 'tsho sna tshogs yangs pa byung //mos pa'i dkyil 'khor mtha' yas stobs bskyed cing //rgyal ba'i mnyam nyid ma nor rtogs byas nas//bzod pa rgya mtsho'i tshul rnams
110b3(phal chen): yongs sbyangs shing //rnam dag kha dog mtha' yas zhing rnams byung //bsod nams sprin ni nam mkha' mnyam bskyed cing //'gro ba kun phan bde don spyod spyad de//bsod nams rgya mtsho sems can kun bstan p+sa//
110b4(phal chen): 'jig rten khams rnams rgya mtsho kun kyang 'byung //zhing rnams sa rgya mtsho'i rdul snyed tshad med pa'i//pha rol phyin pa ma lus kun sbyangs te//smon lam mthar phyin mi zad goms byas
110b5(phal chen): pas//dri med rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams yangs pa 'byung //'byung ba'i spyod pa gang yod tshad med dang //chos rnams thams cad yangs pa yongs sbyangs te//sna tshogs thabs sgos sems can sbyangs
110b6(phal chen): pas na//'di 'dra'i yid 'ongs rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams 'byung //rgyan rnams kun gyi tshul dang sa sbyangs shing //rgyal ba'i yon tan rgya mtsho kun zhugs nas//'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal mtsho skams pas//zhing
110b7(phal chen): gi mtsho yangs 'di kun dag par 'gyur//stobs rnams rgya mtsho gzhal med yongs sbyangs shing //'gro ba 'di 'dra tshad med rnam sbyangs te//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho yangs kun bsnyen bkur bas//zhing rnams rgya
110b8(phal chen): mtsho rnam mang rnam par dag/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho re rer sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul rgya mtsho ni//'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par
111a1(phal chen): @#//bya ste/'di ltar sangs rgyas 'byung ba la la ni sku chung ngur kun tu ston to//'di lta ste/sangs rgyas 'byung ba la la la ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad gzugs kyi skus gang bar ston to//
111a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas 'byung ba la ni sku tshe tshang mi ring ngo //sangs rgyas 'byung ba la la na ni sku tshe'i tshad ring ngo //sangs rgyas byung ba la la ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing cig yongs su dag par byin gyis rlob bo//sangs 
111a3(phal chen): rgyas 'byung ba la la ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su dag par byin kyis rlob bo//sangs rgyas 'byung ba las ni tshul gcig gis bstan pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo byin gyis rlob bo//sang rgyas 
111a4(phal chen): 'byung ba la la ni tshul bsam gyis mi khyab par bstan pa'i chos kyi 'khor la byin gyis rlob bo//sangags rgyas 'byung ba la la ni sems can gyi khams nyung du rnam par gdul ba byin gyis rlob bo//sang
111a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas 'byung ba la las ni sems can rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa rnam par gdul ba byin kyis rlob ste/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams sa rgya mtsho re rer sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 'jig 
111a6(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshur rgya mtsho'i phyogs 'di nyid yongs su bstan pa'i phyir/sangs 
111a7(phal chen): rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba 'byung ba thabs rnams sna tshogs kyi//sgo nas zhing rnams rgya mtshor rab tu 'byung //de dag 
111a8(phal chen): sems can bsam pa ji bzhin mkhyen//sangs rgyas thabs kyi sgo mang rgya mtsho'o//sangs rgyas chos sku bsam gyis mi khyab ste//mig yor ma yin mtshan nyid med pa'i gzugs//gzugs kyi khyad 
111b1(phal chen): par 'gro ba 'di dag tu//sems can bsam pa'i phyogs bzhin ston la ltos//sku tshe thung bar yal rnam par ston//la la bskal pa bsam yas byin gyis rlob//chos kyi sku ni phyogs rnams kun gyi sgo//rab tu 
111b2(phal chen): mdzes par 'gro nang 'di na bzhugs//la la zhing rnams rgya mtsho bsam yas dag/sbyong zhing 'byam klas phyogs bcu kun na yang //la la zhing gcig rnam par sbyong bar mdzad//smon lam ma lus kun kyang 
111b3(phal chen): tshul gcig go/la la bsam gyis mi khyab theg pa'i rgyan//sems can yid bzhin sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//la la theg pa gcig kyang rab ston te//rgyal ba'i thabs ni gcig kyang mtha' yaso//rang 
111b4(phal chen): byung la la byang chub sangs rgyas nas//sems can mi mang la ma la gzhog pa mdzad//la la skad cig gcig gis sems can rnams//rgya mtsho bsam yas byang chub don du 'dul//la 
111b5(phal chen): la spu'i sgo nas bsam yas pa'i//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i sprin rnams rab tu 'gyed//'gro ba kun tu de dag mngon bkang zhing //thabs kyi sgo nas sems can 'dul ba mdzad//la la sangs 
111b6(phal chen): rgyas gsung gis kun bkang zhing //zhing du sems can yid bzhin rab tu sgrog/bsam yas bskal par de dag gsung sgrog cing //sems can rgya mtsho mtha' yas rnam par 'dul//la la zhing rgyan bsam 
111b7(phal chen): gyis mi khyab ste//'khor rnams yongs dag kun nas 'khod bcas pas//zhing mang rgya mtsho phyogs su gang byas shing //sangs rgyas mkha' la sprin ltar khyab par bzhugs//de ltar sangs rgyas thabs 
111b8(phal chen): ni bsam mi khyab//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun tu rgyas bkang zhing //'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas mngon sum ste//sna tshogs rgyan mang du ma'i tshul dang ldan//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams 
112a1(phal chen): @#//rgya mtsho'i bskal pa'i byin gyis brlabs kyi tshul shin tu bstan pa ni//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/'di ltar zhing rgya mtsho la 
112a2(phal chen): la ni bskal pa grangs med par byin gyis brlabs pa'o//zhing rgya mtsho la la ni bskal pa tshad med par byin gyis brlab pa'o//zhing rgya mtsho la ni bskal pa dpag tu med par byin gyis brlabs
112a3(phal chen): pa'o//zhing rgya mtsho la lani bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par byin gyis brlabs pa 'o//zhing rgya mtsho la ni ni bskal pa mtshungs pa med par byin gyis brlab
112a4(phal chen): yas par byin gyis rlabs pa'o//zhing rgya mtshol la ni bskal pa kun nas mu med par byin gyis brlab+sa pa'o//zhing rgya mtsho la ni bskal pa gzhal du med par byin gyis brlab//zhing 
112a5(phal chen): rgya mtsho la la ni bskal ba brjod du med par byin gyis blabs pa ste/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i bskal pa'i byin gyis brlabs kyis tshul shin tu bstan pa rgya 
112a6(phal chen): mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya cho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya tsho'i bskal pa'i byin gyis rlabs kyi tshul shin tu bstan pa 
112a7(phal chen): 'di nyid yongasu bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//'jig rten rgya mtsho bskal pa tha dad de//sna tshogs 
112a8(phal chen): tshul 'jug rgyan rnams gzhan dang gzhan//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na snang //bskal pa'i tshul ni rgya mtsho grangs kyang med//bskal pa mi mthun 'gro ba kun dang mnyam//phyogs 
112b1(phal chen): dbyings 'jig rten rgya mtsho'i khams na snang//bskal pa mtha' yas la la ring dang thung//bskal pa sna tshogs rgyal ba'i sgras kyang bsgrags//rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams 'di dag phyogs dbyings snang//byin rlabs
112b2(phal chen): bskal pa zhing rdul snyed dang ldan//la la gzhan rnams bskal pa gcig gis 'jug/la la bskal grangs smon lam du mas byung//bskal pa la la dri med la la nyon mongs can//la la rnam dag la la nyon
112b3(phal chen): mongs rnam par gnas//bskal pa'i ming ni smon lam rgya mtsho'i tshul gyis byung//sems can 'du shes rnam pas tha dad tshul la 'jug/bskal pa zhing gi rdul snyed sngan chad spyod spyad pas//'jig rten khams
112b4(phal chen): rnams rgya mtsho shin tu yangs shing dag/de dag kun nas sangs rgyas yul rgyan tshad kyang med//'di dag rgya chen bskal pa'i 'jig rten rgya mtsho'i khams//bskal pa'i 'od dang 'od
112b5(phal chen): zer rin chen ming sna tshogs//bcom ldan 'das dang 'od mnyam 'od 'phro'i mig snang ba//snang dang dri med bzang po bskal ba'i ming la 'jug/bskal pa 'di dag rnam dag bskal
112b6(phal chen): pa kun las byung//dri med bskal pa la lar sangs rgyas gcig skye'o//bskal pa la lar mi'i gtso bo mtha' yas 'byung//bskal pa'i tshul rnams thams cad sa tshul zad mi shes//smon lam
112b7(phal chen): stobs mthus bskal pa sna tshogs 'jug par 'gyur//bskal pa la la mtha' yas rnams kyang bskal pa gcig tu 'dus//bskal pa la la gcig kyang bskal pa bye ba'i mthu//bskal pa rgya
112b8(phal chen): mtsho kun kyang sna tshogs sgo nas 'jug/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams kyi nang nas sgra yang 'byin//bskal pa la lar bskal pa kun gyi rgyan kyang 'byung//ming gi rgyan rnams rnam par
113a1(phal chen): @#//dag pa'ang de la yod//bskal pa la la sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul mthu yis byung//bskal pa'i tshul rnams kun gyi 'di ni rgyan yangs yin//zhing dang bskal pa rgya mtsho tshul ni 
113a2(phal chen): gcig gis dag/zhing gi rdul rnams kun kyang tshad med gzhal du med//skad cig yud tsam nyi ma'i grangs kyi bskal par 'gyur//'di dag thams cad sems can 'du shes rnam par 'byung//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 
113a3(phal chen): de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag bskal pas mtha' yas pa'i rgyud kyi tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/'di ltar chos nyid rab tu thob pas 'jig 
113a4(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag pa bskal pas 'jig cing 'du ba la rab tu 'jug par gnas pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tshor gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pas bskal pa mtha'
113a5(phal chen): yas pa kun nas gnas pa dang /'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgyam tshor gtogs pa'i sems pa rnams kyi bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen po bsags pa/rnam par dag pas 'jig 
113a6(phal chen): rten gyi khamasargya mtsho kun nas nyon mongs la rnam par dag par gnas pa dang /'id lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' mos pa sna tshogs kyis spyod pa rnams skye bas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso rnam par dag la
113a7(phal chen): kun nas nyon mongs par gnas pa dang /'di ltar ste/sems can rgya mtsho rgya chen po byang chub kyi sems la gnas pas 'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho shin tu dag par gnas pa dang/'di lta ste/byang chub sems
113a8(phal chen): dpa' rnams 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang gzhan du 'dong bas bskal pa rgyan tha dad pa'i tshul du      gnas pa dang /'di ltaste/phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshor 
113b1(phal chen): 'dus pas/bskal pa mtha' yas pa rgyan shin tu mang por kun nas gnas pa dang /'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa yongasu mya ngan las 'da' bas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho dag gi rgyan nub par
113b2(phal chen): 'gyur ba dang /'di lta ste/sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshe 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho rnams rgyan gyis brgyan par kun nas gnas pa dang/'di lta ste/de bzhin ga shegs pa rnam par 'phrul bas 'jig rten gyi khams
113b3(phal chen): rgya mtsho'i bskal pa mtha' yas pa yongasu dag par gnas pa dang /de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i rgyud kyi tshul rgya mtsho kun nas gnas pa'i tshul ni
113b4(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshe'i bskal pa mtha' yas pa'i
113b5(phal chen): tshul rgya mtsho 'di nyid yongasu bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byi na gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i chos
113b6(phal chen): nyid 'di dag ltos//chos kyis byung ste ji ltar gnas ba ltar//de dag 'byung zhing 'jig par 'gyur bar yang //zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu snang //sems can nyon mongs mi bzad ldan
113b7(phal chen): pa dang /las dang nyon mongs ngan bas bsgribs pa'i rnams//de dag bsam la zhing gi rgya mtsho rnams//kun nas nyon mongs ldan par gyur pa yin//sems can rnam dag bsam pa ldan pa dang //
113b8(phal chen): bsod nams brtan dang rnam dag spyod yul can//rnam dag yul la nyon mongs ldan gyur rnams//de dag mos pa sna tshogs spyod yul gyi//byang chub sems dpa' skye ba'i dus dag na//bskal
114a1(phal chen): pa de ni rnam par dag par snang //sems can nyon mongs ldan par gyur pa yang //de'i chos nyid bsam yas yangs pa yi//byang chub bsam pa ji ltar bskyed pa dang //
114a2(phal chen): de dag bsam pa ji ltar rnam 'phel bas//de bzhin bskal pa rgya mtsho mang po dag/bsam gyis mi khyab zhing rgyan yongs su sbyong //bye ba mang po mtha' yas mi zad pa'i//byang chub sems dpa' phyogs bcu kun rgyu 
114a3(phal chen): yang //rgyan la rnam pa tha dad yod pa min//bskal pa tha dad tshul gyi bye phrag snang //rdul rnams re re sbubs su dus kun tu//sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul dang mnyam pa yi//rgyal sras ji tshul 'dod pa de bzhin du//
114a4(phal chen): zhing rnams rgya mtsho de tshul dag par gyur//gal te rgyal ba rnams ni skye 'gyur na//zhing rnams rgyan gyis shin tu brgyan par 'gyur//bsam pa yongs dag de bzhin rgyas 'gyur te//
114a5(phal chen): 'di ni zhing rgyan phun sum tshogs pa'o//gal te rgyal ba mya ngan 'das 'gyur na//de tshe de zhing rgyan rnams 'grib par 'gyur//sems can rnams kyang snod du rung ba min//zhing gi 
114a6(phal chen): rgya mtsho de yang nyon mongs ldan//sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul rnam 'phrul yangs pa dag/bsam gyis mi khyab gang tshe ston pa na//de tshe zhing rnams rgya mtsho mang po dag/kun kyang rgyan gyis rnam par 
114a7(phal chen): brgyan par 'gyur//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa'i tshul ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom 
114a8(phal chen): ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//de ni 'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho gcig tu 
114b1(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams tha mi dad pa dang /'di lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso dag tu/sangs rgyas skye zhing 'byung ba tha mi dad pa dang /'dilta ste/
114b2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho dag gi snying po byang chub thams cad nas/chos kyi dbyings kyis phyogs rgyas par 'gengs pa tha mi dad pa dang /'dil lta ste/'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho dag gi de bzhin gash+ye
114b3(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor tha mi dad pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya mo dag tu sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sprul pa'i sbrin dang /mtshan gyi 'khor lo tha mi dad pa dang /'ad lta ste/'jig rten gyi khamam rgya mtsho dag 
114b4(phal chen): tu sangs rgyas thams cad dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor bskal pa mtha' yas par brjod cing rgyas par bya ba'i byin gyi rlabs tha mi dad pa dang /'id lta ste/'jig rten gyi kams rgya mtsho dag 
114b5(phal chen): tu chos kyi 'khor lo'i tshul tha mi dad pa dang /'de lta ste/'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho dag tu rdul shin tu phra ba gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su 'du ba tha mi dad pa dang /'a'adi lta ste/
114b6(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho dag tu rdul 'byam klas pa thams cad la dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi yul rgya chen po ston cing rjod pa tha mi dad pa dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de dag la sogs pa 
114b7(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho tha mi dad pa'i tshul/'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams
115a1(phal chen): @#//rgya mtsho tha mi dad pa'i tshul rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa 'di nyid gsal bar bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su 
115a2(phal chen): bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rdul gcig steng na zhing mang rgya mtsho ste//kun na rgyan mang rnam dag kun yod kyang //gnod pa med cing 'dre ba gang yang med//'jug pa bsam gyis mi khyab 'di la ltos//rdul rnams 
115a3(phal chen): kun la bsam yas sangs rgyas bzhugs//sems can kun gyi bsam pa yid bzhin du//zhing rnams rgya mtsho thams cad khyab byas kyang //de dag thabs la tha dad gang yang med//rdul rnams kun la shing 
115a4(phal chen): dbang mtha' yas pa//sna tshogs rgyun phyung kun nas bres sbya ngas te//phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing du mngon bkang yang //de dag tha dad 'gyur ba nam yang med//rdul phran rgya mtsho rdul phran re re 
115a5(phal chen): la//mi'i gtso bo 'khor rnams kun bskor cing //'jig rten kun tu bkang ste 'phags bzhugs kyang //gnod pa dag dang 'dre ba'i nyes pa med//rdul gcig la ni mtha' yas 'od kyi 
115a6(phal chen): sprin//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun bkang zhing //bde gshegs rnams kyi byang chub spyod bstan kyang //rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams tha dad gang yang med/rdul rnams kun la kha dog bsam mi khyab//
115a7(phal chen): sprul pa'i sprin gyis kun tu gang byas te//sangs rgyas sprul pas sems can rnams 'dul yang //'byam klas phyogs na tha dad pa gang yang med//rdul rnalas kun la rgya mtsho'i chos brjod de//de dag 
115a8(phal chen): 'khor lo tshul bzhin 'khor mdzad cing //thabs dang mthu'i sgo mo sna tshogs kyis//thams cad brjod kyang de la tha dad med//rdul gcig la ni/rgyal ba kun gyis gsung //'gro ba snod du gyur la 
115b1(phal chen): de dag brjod byas te//rgya mtsho'i zhing du bskal bar byin brlabs kyang//de dag gsung gi yan lag tha mi dad//zhing rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas rgyan gyi rnams//thams cad rdul sbubs gcig tu rab tu
115b2(phal chen): 'jud//rgyal ba rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul mthu la ltos//las kyi dngos las de dag skyes pa min//rdul gcig la ni dus gsum rgyal ba dag/ji ltar byung ba de dag thams cad snang//de dag gshegs dang
115b3(phal chen): byon pa gang yang med//'gro ba kun na sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa'i phyogs
115b4(phal chen): gsal bar bya ba yang dag par bsgrubs pa zhes bya ba ste/le'u bzhi pa'o////bam po brgyad pa//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig
115b5(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan gyis brgyan pa 'di bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe/bskal
115b6(phal chen): pa rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis yongasu dag par byas te/bskal pa re re zhing yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i jag rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyid kyi zhabs
115b7(phal chen): kyi drung du smon lam rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi kham rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu pra ba snyed kyis yongasu dag pa ra byaso//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang//'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tag gi rgyan
115b8(phal chen): gyis brgyan pa de ni rlung gi dkyil 'khor ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed gyis shin tu bzung ba ste/de la rlung gi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi 'og ma ni/gzhi kun nas rton pa zhes bya ste/rin po che
116a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad kyi 'od 'phro ba 'bar bas brgyan pa'i gzhis rton to//de'i steng na rlung gi dkyil 'khor rin po che sna tshogs kyi rgyan 'byung ba zhes bya ba yod de/nor bu'i 
116a2(phal chen): rgyal po 'od rnam par dag pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi gzhis ston to//de'i steng na rlungagi dkyil 'khor rin po che'i mthu zhes bya ba yod de/rin po che thams cad kyi dril bu'i gzhis rton to//de'i steg na rlung 
116a3(phal chen): gi dkyil 'khor 'od 'phro mnyam par rton pa zhes bya ba yod de/nor ba'i rgyal po nyi ma snang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi 'khor lo'i gzhis rton to//de'i ste na rlung gi dkyil 'khorargyan kun nas 
116a4(phal chen): rnam par bres pa zhes bya ba yod de/'od zer gyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi me tog gi gzhis rton to//de'i sten na rlung gi dkyil 'khoragazhi kun nas dri ma med cing yongasu 
116a5(phal chen): dag pa zhes bya ba yod de/me tog thams cad 'od 'phro ba'i seng ge'i khri'i gzhis rton to//de'i ster na rlung gi dkyil 'khoraphyogasu sgra sgrog cing kun tu rgyu ba zhes bya ba yod de/
116a6(phal chen): mu tig dang man shel gyi rgyal mtshan sna tshogs kyi gzhis rton to//de'i steng na rlung gi dkyil 'khor rin po che thams cad 'dzin pa zhes bya ba yod de/nor bu'i rgyal po'i shing dang me tog sna tshogs 
116a7(phal chen): kyi glong gi gzhis rton to//de'isteng na rlung gi dkyil 'khaur kun nas 'dzin pa'i shugs zhes bya ba yod de/spos kyi nor bu thams cad kyi ri rab kyi sprin gyi gzhis rton to//de'i steg na rlung gi dkyil 
116a8(phal chen): 'khor khyim kun tu rgyu ba zhes bya ba yid de/rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog dang /spos kyi khang pa brtsegs pa'i sprin gyi gzhis rton to//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/rlung gi dkyil 'khor ri rab 
116b1(phal chen): kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed de dag thams cad kyi steng ma ni rlung gi dkyil 'khor gzi brjid bzang po dpal gyi snying po zhes bya ste/nor bu kun nas snang ba'i rgyan dang ldan pa'i spos kyi chu rgya mtsho'i gzhis rton to//
116b2(phal chen): spos kyi chu rgya mtsho nor bu kun nas snang ba'i gzhis brgyan pa'i snying po zhes bya ba de las pad+mo chen po spos kyi rgyal mtshan khyer ba'i 'od zer dang /ze ba sna tshogs shes bya ba mngon par 'byung ste/pad+mo chen 
116b3(phal chen): po de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan gyi brgyan pa kun nas gru bzhir 'dug pa/rnam par dag pa/shin tu brtan pa/rdo rje dang nor bu thams cad kyi khor yug gis bskor ba/
116b4(phal chen): rim gyis gzhol bar gnas pa/shing gi rgyud rgya mtsho sa la rab tu gnas pa 'dug go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya matso gzhi dang snying po me 
116b5(phal chen): tog gi rgyan pa 'di nyid kyi yon tan rgya mtsho yongs su dag pa'i phyogs 'di nyid brjod pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe 
116b6(phal chen): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//gzhi rgyan me tog 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgya mtsho de//rin chen kun gyi snying po kha dog sna tshogs te//rdul rnams kun dang mnyam pa'i sngan chad sangs 
116b7(phal chen): rgyas kyi//drung du sngon tshe rgya mtshor sbyangs pas dri ma med//rgya chen snying rje'i sprin gyis khams rnams kun bkang zhing //zhing gi rdul dang mnyam pa'i lus kyang kun btang nas//sngon gyi 
116b8(phal chen): bskal pa rgya mtshor spyod pa spyad pas na//'jig rten rgya mtsho'i khams de yangs shing rnam par dag/sangs rgyas rnam par snang bas smon lam che mdzad de//'od snang gtang ba mkha'i 
117a1(phal chen): @#//dkyil na gnas pa ni//rlung gi phung pos btab kyang mi skyod mi g.yo ste//nor bu kun rgyan sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan snying po'o//me tog mdzod bkye gtor ba rin chen yid 
117a2(phal chen): 'ong bzhin//sngon gyi smon lam dbang gis mkha'i dbyings la gnas//rgya mtsho'i rgyan rnams thams cad sna tshogs brtan par gnas//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams kun tu 'od kyi sprin kyang gtong //
117a3(phal chen): nor bu thams cad sgo nas byang chub sems dpa'i rnam//byung zhing 'od zer sprin 'bar phyogs rnams kun tu 'dong //'od zer me tog tshon dang 'od zer 'khor lo'i tshogs//phyogs rnams ma lus kun gyi chos 
117a4(phal chen): dbyings khyab par byed//rin chen kun gyis 'od zer sprin rnams bkye ba ni//phyogs dang phyogs mtsham kun gyi zhing rnams khyab par byed//sdug bsngal thams cad bcil zhing byang chub 
117a5(phal chen): mchog la 'god//'jig rten rgya mtsho thams cad 'od 'phro rab tu 'bar//'gro ba kun dang mnyam pa'i sprul pa sna tshogs rnams//rin chen kun dang spu rnams kun nas rgyal ba 'gyed//
117a6(phal chen): brgya byin dag dang tshangs pa'ang 'khor lo sgyu 'dra dang //'gro ba kun gyi lus su lus rnams kun kyang gnas//sprul pas 'od bkye chos kyi dbyings kyi tshad mnyam pa//'od zer sgo nas sangs rgyas thams 
117a7(phal chen): cad mtshan kyang sgrog/'gro ba ma lus kun gyi bsam pa tshim byas nas//tshul rnams sna tshogs sgo nas sems can rnam par 'dul//'jig rten rgya mtsho me tog snying po de nang na//rdul phran kun gyi khongs na

117b1(phal chen): te//smon lam yangs pa'i sprin ni chos kyi dbyings mnyam dang //bzang po'i spyod pa brnyes shing ye shes sa ldan pa'i//dgongs pa las ni rgyan mdzes de dag byung ba yin//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs 
117b2(phal chen): rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho yongs su dag pas snang ba zhes bya ba ste/le'u lnga pa'o////kye 
117b3(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho gzhi dang snying po me toggi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i khor yug chen po ni nor bu'i rgyal po pad+mo nyi ma'i gzhi la brten pa ste/nor bu'i rigs rgya mtsho spos kyi tsan 
117b4(phal chen): dan thams cad las grub pa nor bu'i rgyal po rgyan gyi yul thams cad kyis lhun shin tu 'phags shing zil gyis gnon pa nor bu'i spos zhim po gzi brjid kyi 'khor lo rnam par snang ba'i snying po 
117b5(phal chen): can/nor bu'i rgyal po rdo rje sra zhing mi shigs pa ltar shin tu mkhregs shing sra ba rin po che'i chu thams cad kun nas 'byung ba rin po che'i shing gi nags tshal gyi rgyan thams cad me tog 
117b6(phal chen): shin tu rgyas pa/spos kyi rtsi mchog bzang po thams cad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/spos kyi me tog dpal can shin tu bzang bas rnam par brgyan pa/nor bu'i rgyal po 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi 
117b7(phal chen): dbyibs lta bu'i dra bas kun nas bres pa ste/de dag la sogs pa rgyan bzang po'i rnam pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba bsnyed kyis rab tu brgyan pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu 
117b8(phal chen): bzang pos yang 'khor yug gi rgyan dang /yon tan yongs su dag pa 'di nyid bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//
118a1(phal chen): 'jig rten khams rnams rgya mtsho gang yod pa//mtha' dang dbung med sna tshogs rnam pa can//nor bu mang pos rnam dag mtha' yas mdog/de dag sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul bas byung //
118a2(phal chen): dri med khor yug rgyan gyis brgyan pa ni//nor bu mang po de bzhin spos mang dang //mu tig gsal pa'i tshogs la rab tu gnas//kha dog mtha' yas rin chen rnams kyis brtan//snying po nor bu'i sprin sna 
118a3(phal chen): tshogs sra//'dzam bu chu klung 'od 'phro snying po'i tshogs//'od 'phro dam pa phyogs su snang zhing 'tsher//khor yug dri ma med pa rnam par mdzes//nor bu rdo rjes 'dus byas shin tu sra shing brtan//mtshams

118a5(phal chen): tsan dan me tog kun tu bgram//pad+mo sna tshogs gos kyis kun nas yog/rin chen rtsi mchog dri zhim rnams dang ldan//sna tshogs shing gi sprin gyis kun nas brgyan//nor bzang kun 
118a6(phal chen): gyis kun nas gzi brjid 'bar//'od zer sprin gyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor can//gos kyi mdzod kyis shin tu 'bar bar gnas//spos kyi 'od 'phro bdug pa'i gdugs 'chang ba'i//byang chub sems

118a8(phal chen): 'od 'phro'i shing //kun la rnam par snang mdzad sku gsal ba//gzugs brnyan tshul tu nang na yod pa snang //thams cad gzugs su sku rnams mtha' yas te//sangs rgyas sprul mang kha dog mu med 
118b1(phal chen): pa//thams cad phyogs dang chos kyi dbyings bkang zhing//sems can mang po tshad med rnam par 'dul//thams cad rgyan las sgra dbyangs kun 'byung ste//rnam par snang mdzad smon lam 'khor lo 
118b2(phal chen): yis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho yongs su dag byas pa//de kun de las rnam par 'phrul zhing grag/shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i
118b3(phal chen): khor yug gi rgyan rgya mtsho shin tu bstan pa zhes bya ba ste/le'u drug pa'o////kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la khor yug chen po de'i nang ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i
118b4(phal chen): sa chen po'i gzhi ni//nor bu mang po'i rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa/rdo rje ltar shin tu 'khregs shing sra la brtan pa/rin po che'i rigs thams cad las grub pa/rgya mtshos rnam
118b5(phal chen): par phye ba mi shigs pa'i snying po can/mi mthong mi mda' ba'i thang mnyam zhing rnam par dag pa/nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog dang 'od thams cad kyis rab tu brgyan pa//
118b6(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi lus kyi dbyibs lta bu'i nor bu'i gzugs sna tshogs kyis rab tu bsgrubs pa/rin po che'i tshon thams cad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/rin po che yid du 'ong ba'i pad+mo thams
118b7(phal chen): cad kyis gzhi byas pa/dmigs pa thams cad du sna tshogasu gyur ba'i sprin gyis kun nas yog pa/nor bu'i rgyal po spos kyi mdzod thams cad kyis gzhi mnyam par bdal ba/nor bu'i rgyal po 
118b8(phal chen): dus gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhing gi rgyan gyi sprin gyi dra ba rnam par bres pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul gyi 'od dang /nor bu'i dra ba mig 'phrul lta bu rnam par phye ba ste/kye

119a3(phal chen): dkyil 'khor gzhi ni rnam dag mnyam//'khor lo'i dra bas bsgrubs pas mi shigs sra//rin chen sna tshogs mnyam pa'i gzhir gnas shing //rin chen sna tshogs kun gyis kun tu gang //yid 'ong sa ni

119a5(phal chen): rnams//'od 'phro 'gyed pas phyogs su sprin kyang 'bar//sangs rgyas 'od snang nor bu rnam par bkram//sa kun na me tog yid du 'ong bas yog/thams cad sprin ni phyogs na

119a7(phal chen): smon lam kun//shin tu dus ring rtag tu kun tu ston//sngan chad spyod pa ji 'dra spyod pa dag/kun kyang nor bu'i 'bru las gsal bar snang //de'i rgya mtsho nor bu sna tshogs gting //de'i nang na sangs rgyas 
119a8(phal chen): spyod yul snang //sangs rgyas spyod yul de dag zhing gi rnams//rgya mtsho kun gyi rdul phran re rer snang //me tog snying po tshon gyi 'khor los bsgrubs//der ni byang chub sems dpa' rtag 'dong zhing //
119b1(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam kun kyang sgrog/snying po byang chub 'khor du rnam 'phrul lo//rin chen 'od zer 'od dang ldan pa dag/sa'i nor bu rgyan las rab tu 'byung //sangs rgyas chos nyid 'di yang rtag tu 
119b2(phal chen): ston//chos dbyings nam mkha' lta bu kun tu 'gengs//sangs rgyas kun yul ye shes rgya chen dang //kun tu bzang po'i smon lam sus shes pa//de dag phyogs rnams rgya mtsho kun tu yang //zhing

119b4(phal chen): le'u bdun pa'o////kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i gzhi sa'i dkyil 'khor de la spos chu'i rgya mtsho sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa'i 
119b5(phal chen): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed yod de//de dag kun kyang gzhi rin po che thams cad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'o//nor bu'i rgyal po dang spos thams cad kyis klung rab tu bsgrubs pa'o//nor bu'i 
119b6(phal chen): rgyal po bzang zhing yid du 'ong ba'i 'od rnam par snang ba'i dra bas kun nas bres pa'o//rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog dang mchog tu snang ba'i spos kyi chus yongs su rgyas pa'o//rin po che thams
119b7(phal chen): cad kyi me tog sna tshogs klong du khyil pa'o//tsan dan gyi phye ma dang 'dres pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gi dkyil 'khor dang /smon lam rgya mtsho'i sgra 'byin pa'o//rin po che thams 
119b8(phal chen): cad kyi 'od zer gyi dkyil 'khor gyi sprin rnam par snang ba'o//byang chub sems dpa'i lus kyi sprin me tog sna tshogs kyi kha dog lta bur rab tu 'gyed pa'o//kha dog dang dbyibs mtha' yas pa'i 
120a1(phal chen): gdugs kyis khebs par rnam par 'phrul bas brgyan pa'o//dmigs pa thams cad la 'jig rten gyi khams mang po rgya chen po thams cad snang ba'o//phyogs kun nas rin po 
120a2(phal chen): che'i them skas kyi phreng ba bcur rab tu bkye ba'o//mu tig rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog lta bu'i mu khyud kyis kun nas bskor ba'o//rin po che shel gyi rgyal mtshan bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa bcus 
120a3(phal chen): kun nas brgyan pa'o//'di lta ste/nor bu dang me tog gi khang pa brtsegs pa chu klung gang+gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kha dog mtha' yas pa bcus kun nas bskor ba'o/'di lta ste/nor bu thams cad kyi pad+mo yid du 'ong 
120a4(phal chen): ba'i grong khyer bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcus kun nas bskor ba'o //'di lta ste/nor bu rdo rje shin tu sra ba gling bzhi'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis rab tu bsgrubs pa'o//
120a5(phal chen): rin po che thams cad kyi me tog pun da ri ka dang /spos kyi chu yong su rgyas pas kun nas bskor ba'o/'i shing gi phreng ba rin po che 'bar zhing kun nas 'phyang ba'i nor bu'i

162a1(phal chen): par 'phrul pa dang de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs gzhan dang gzhan can sha stag go/'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud de dag re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi mtshams nas rab tu byung ba'i 
162a2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams kyi dra ba rgyan sna tshogs la rab tu gnas pa thams cad kyis 'jig rten gyi khams rnam pa sna tshogs kyi tshul du 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang /snying po me tog gi rgyan gyis brgyan pa 
162a3(phal chen): zhes bya ba 'di thams cad yongs su gang ngo //ji ltar 'jig rten gyi khams gcig gi rgyud nas byung ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyu rnams dang /'jig rten gyi khams kyis mtshams dang /sgo 
162a4(phal chen): rnam pa sna tshogs kyi rgyan la gnas pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyis dra ba sna tshogs thams cad las byung ba kun gyis kyang /'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan gyi 
162a5(phal chen): brgyan pa 'di thams cad yongs su gong ngo //de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams kyi brgyud mtha' dang dbung med pa dang /'jig rten gyi kham 
162a6(phal chen): 'byam klas pa'i tshul shin tu bstan pa 'di nyid gsal bar brjod pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//'jig 
162a7(phal chen): rten khams ni rgya mtsho'i rnams//yangs dang mi yangs sna tshogs pa//nam mkha' dag gi dbyings la gnas//chos kyi dbyings ni tha mi dad//'jig rten khams 'di rgya mtsho na//
162a8(phal chen): zhing gi rgyud ni bsam mi khyab//tha dad min par rnam par gnas//rnam 'phrul gzhan dang gzhan dag go/rnam phye sna tshogs shin tu gnas//kha dog tha dad rab tu 'dug/zhing gi rgyud dag rnam phye ba//
162b1(phal chen): 'jig rten     khams kyi gangs chen mtsho//lhun dang dbyangs rnams tha dad de//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnam 'phrul ba//zhing gi rgyud rnams rab tu snang//las kyis de dag shin tu bris//ri rab rgya mtsho'i
162b2(phal chen): dra ba dang //'khor lo glong gi dkyil 'khor dang //pad mo'i dbyibs 'dra 'byam klas pa//de dag de yis kun nas bskor//rdo'i rgyal mtshan brtsegs pa dang //rdo rje kun nas 'khor gnas pa//
162b3(phal chen): 'byam klas bsam gyis mi khyab pas//de dag rgyud ni kun nas bskor//'od kyi dra ba bsam mi khyab//mu tig mdog kyang rgya mtsho ste//zhing gi rgyud rnams kun kyang 'bar//pad mo la yang kun gnaso//
162b4(phal chen): re re dag gi rgyud las kyang//'od zer dra ba bsam mi khyab//'o Ida zer dag gi zhing gyur cing//rgya mtsho phyogs rnams kun tu 'gengs//zhing gi rgyud rnams thams cad kyi//dmigs 
162b5(phal chen): pa kun gyi sgo nas kyang//thams cad tshul la 'jug pa yis//mi zad tshul rnams rab tu snang//zhing gi rnams ni bsam mi khyab//rgyud rnams mi zad shin tu 'byung//rgyan rnams
162b6(phal chen): bstan pa tha dad te//drang srong chen po'i byin rlabaso//zhing gi rgyud rnams de dag na//'jig rten bsam gyis mi khyab khams//kun nas 'du zhing rnam par 'jig/'byung zhing 'jig par de bzhin gyur/
162b7(phal chen): ji ltar shing la lo ma'i rnams//'byung zhing ltung bar 'gyur ba ltar//de bzhin zhing gi rgyud rnams na//'jig rten khams rnams 'byung ba yin//ji ltar shing gi skye gnas nas//shing tog sna
162b8(phal chen): tshogs 'byung ba ltar//de bzhin zhing gyi rgyud rnams na//sems can sna tshogs 'byung ba yin//ji ltaras bon khyad par gyis//'bras bu sna tshogs snang ba ltar//de bzhin las kyi khyad
163a1(phal chen): par gyis//sems can rnams dang zhing byung ngo //yid bzhin rin chen rgyal po'i rnams//kha dog kun tu snang ba ltar//sems can bsam pa rnam dag pas//de 
163a2(phal chen): bzhin zhing rnams rab tu snang //klu'i rgyal po sprin gyi rnams//nam mkha'i dbyings la ston pa ltar//de bzhin sangs rgyas smon lam gyis//zhing gi rnams ni 'byung ba yin//sgyu ma'i mkhan po de sngags 
163a3(phal chen): dag gis//sgo rnams sna tshogs ston pa ltar//de bzhin sems can las rnams kyis//bsam gyis mi khyab zhing 'byung ngo //ji ltar ri mo'i gzugs kyi rnams//ri mo'i las kyis bris pa ltar//
163a4(phal chen): de bzhin zhing rnams 'di dag kun//sems kyis ri mor bris pa yin//sems can lus kyi rnam pa dag/ji ltar sems kyis brtags pa ltar//de bzhin du ni zhing gi mtshams//
163a5(phal chen): thams cad las kyis rnam par bris//ji ltar gzhan dang gzhan gzugs su//sems can rnams kyi bsam pa bzhin//mgon po rnams ni snang ba ltar//de bzhin zhing rnams 
163a6(phal chen): snang ba'o//pad mo'i dra ba'i phyi rol gyis//sems can zhing gi mtshams dag na//dbyibs dang kha dog gzhan dang gzhan//dri ma med pa sna tshogs snang //pad mo'i dra ba de dag la//zhing gi dra ba 
163a7(phal chen): rab tu gnas//rgyan dang bstan pa sna tshogs te//sems can sna tshogs mang po'i gnas//zhing gi dbyings rnams la la ni//kun nas nyon mongs rnam par gnas//sems can rnams kyis 
163a8(phal chen): nyon mongs las//chos nyid de dag nang na snang //kun nas nyon mongs rna dag tu//zhing gi rgyud ni bsam mi khyab//sems can bsam pas rab tu byung //byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
163b1(phal chen): kyang 'khod//la la rnam dag nyon mongs can//zhing gi dbyings ni rnam pa mang//byang chub sems dpas rnam par sprul//rgya mtsho'i las las byung ba yin//zhing rnams la la'i 'od
163b2(phal chen): btang ba//rin chen rang bzhin dri ma med//rnam pa sna tshogs dri med dag/sangs rgyas rnams kyis yongasu sbyangs//zhing gi rgyud rnams la la na//bskal pa'i me ni bsam mi khyab//
163b3(phal chen): kun nas nyon mongs rnams la ston//zhing gi dkyil 'khor shin tu sra//rlung gi phung po smon lam dang //chu'i phung la rab tu gnas//zhing gi rnam pa shin tu mang//sems can las kyis bsag+sa
163b4(phal chen): pa yin//zhing na gzhan dang gzhan dag gi/zhing gi chos nyid snang la ltos//de dag 'byung ba gang yang med//de dag la ni 'jig pa'ang med//skad cig skad cig sems kun la//sangs

163b6(phal chen): med cing 'od kyang med//sdig pa'i las kyi gnas yin no//zhing rnams la la rdo rje'i dngos//kun nas nyon mongs 'jigs pa che//bde ba chung zhing sdug bsngal mang//bsod nams
163b7(phal chen): chung ba'i gnas kyang yin//zhing rnams la la lcags kyi dngos//la la zangs kyi dngosu gnas//sdig pa'i las kyis kun tu gang//ri dang brag gis mnyam pa med//'jig rten de na dmyal ba yi//
163b8(phal chen): sems can sdug bsngal skyabs med pa//mun pa rjesu rnyed gyur cing//me lce rgya mtsho'i nang na 'bar//'on kyang byol song skye gnasu//lus ni mngon par 'byung gyur te//bdag gi las kyi
164a1(phal chen): @#//nyes pa yis//sdug bsngal tshor ba rab tu myong //gshin rje'i 'jig rten rnams na'ang snang //skom dang bkres pas rab tu mnar//me yi ri yis gdung ba yi//sdug

164a8(phal chen): 'od kyang rab tu 'gyed//'od zer dra bas snang bar byas//zhing rnams la la rdo rje dang //pad mo'i dngos su spos la gnas//la la nor bu'i 'od kyang yin//zhing rnams yongs su dag la ltos//

164b8(phal chen): rgya mtsho rnam mang bas//zhing rnam la la rnam par sbyangs//de dag rgyan mang thams cad du//zhing gi rgya mtsho shin tu snang//kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gyis//zhing rnams yongasu gang

167b1(phal chen): las me tog ri rab me tog 'od 'phro ba'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba rin po che'i khri 'phang bcu'i 'khor gyis bskor cing brgyan pa zhig byung ste/ri rab de'i steng na nags tshal chen po yal ga'i dkyil 'khor rin po

228a4(phal chen): dang sems la rnam par rtog med cing //bde gshegs chos la gang zhig 'jug 'gyur ba//de dag chos rnams kun gyi rang bzhin la//nam yang nem nur dbang du mi 'gro'o//'jig rten dag na dmigs 
228a5(phal chen): pa gang yod pa//de dag kun la bstan pa'i chos 'jug ste//gang dag phyir rgol med pas bstan mdzad kyang //rang bzhin ldog par 'gyur pa gang yang med//dngos po rab shes las rnams yod 
228a6(phal chen): min par//the tsom thams cad rnam par spangs byas te//sangs rgyas byang chub rab tu rtog pa'i tshe//sems la rnam par 'byed pa nam yang med//mi shes chos rnams rnam par phye ba dag/byung med tshig gi 
228a7(phal chen): lam gyis rab brjod pa//sangs rgyas gsung gi rjes su 'brang byas na//gcig kyang ma yin mang po'ang ma yin no//mang dang gcig pa nam yang yod min te//mang po rnams la gcig pa yong med do//
228a8(phal chen): mang dang gcig pa rnam par bsgom byas na//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas shes par 'gyur//'jig rten kun tu sems can zhing rnams dang //shin tu zhi ba'i chos rnams rnam bltas te//mi gnas 
228b1(phal chen): sems la rtogs pa med pa yi//dran ldan sangs rgyas byang chub thob par 'gyur//sems can chos dang de bzhin zhing gi rnams//de dag rang bzhin ji bzhin brtags byas na//gcig dang tha dad gyur pa ma yin te//
228b2(phal chen): de ltar sangs rgyas tshul 'di shes pa yin//de nas 'od gzer de dag 'jig rten gyi khams de dag nas 'das te//shar phyogs logasu 'jig rten gyi khams bye ba snang bar byas pa nas/steng gi phyogs kyi 'jig 
228b3(phal chen): rten gyi khams bye ba'i bar du snang bar byas te/'jig rten gyi khams re rer yang 'dzam bu'i gling bye ba phrag brgya bstan pa nas/'og min bye ba phrag brgya'i bar du bstan to//'jig rten gyi khams de dag

229a1(phal chen): pa mtshan 'di zhes bya ba dag gi zham ring na/tshangs pa'i spyad pa spyod pa dag ste/'di ltar rig byed mi g.yo ba zhes bya ba nas/rig byed sna tshogs shes bya ba'i bar dag go/byang chub sems

229a4(phal chen): gyur to//shes rab dpag med chos rnams mtha' yas pa//srid pa'i rgya mtsho'i pha rol phyin pa dag/tshe ring nad med mnyam med mtshungs med 'od//yon tan che mnga' de dag sbyor 'di'o//

229a6(phal chen): sems can yul mos sems can 'du shes med//'jig tshogs chos shes lam gyi rgyud dang bral//bsam gtan nang dad sems la bcings pa med//chags med yid ldan de dag sbyor 'di yin//

230b2(phal chen): la bag yod gang spyod cing //bdag gis sems can kun bsgral de dag spyod yul 'di//mngon mtha' shes dka' tshad med brtan pas mthong gyur cing //bdag rten sems can pha rol mtha' yas 'khor gyur

235a1(phal chen): bzhin de bzhin no//ji ltar rin chen mchog rnams dang //lha'i bu rnams kun ldan yang //'pho bar snang ba gang yang med//de bzhin las kyi rang bzhin no//ji ltar 'jig rten la
